### Saved C01 diagnostic inventory — paused

`docs/review/c01-fore-trim-probe.mjs` is read-only diagnosis: `visit` collects exact published shield triangles/B1 floor points; `convexHull` derives the fore storage floor envelope (`cross`/`half` are its local orientation/chain helpers); `insideRoom` independently checks point inclusion and actual edge distance. Top-level sampling reports triangles strictly26cm inside fore floor/enclosure outlines and existing shield topology. `docs/review/c01-fore-trim-baseline.{json,log}` contain diagnostic data, no functions. This does not repair geometry or establish route acceptance.

`docs/review/c01-fore-trim-fit-study.mjs` computes a candidate in memory only. `inset` offsets the retained contour; `prisms` triangulates the exact contour into bounded half-space volumes; `subtract` retains outside polygons; `first` independently raycasts original/candidate triangles; `compare` checks exterior first-hit location/material. It exports no model and edits no source/master/publication. `c01-fore-trim-fit-study.{json,log}` record candidate clearance/exterior preservation, with rejection required if the exterior guard changes. The diagnostic module exports its already collected `pieces`, `volumes`, `insideRoom` for this study only.

`docs/review/c01-bow-exterior-baseline.json` preserves all688 original exported-shield ray origins/directions, first-hit positions/materials and original publication hash for a later independent regression guard. Generated solely from the existing publication before C01 implementation; JSON data, no functions.

`docs/review/c01-fore-review.mjs` — prepared matched before/after appearance capture; top-level reads the current publication and17 fore-room/closet/exterior poses. `toThree` converts the documented Blender frame; browser callback `followShipForC01` follows the real ship transform; `capture` records actual PNG/first-hit metadata without hiding meshes or placing a player. Accepts only `before`/`after`, writes `c01-{before,after}-*.png` and `c01-{before,after}-views.json` with publication hash and errors. `docs/review/c01-fore-trim-candidate.py` — unpublished authoring prototype, not imported by `tools` or the application. `room_masks` builds exact-contour bounded triangular prisms; `retain_outside` clips with double-precision tuple arithmetic; `trim_shield` preserves source-triangle materials/winding/world placement while removing only selected interior pieces; `trim_fore_decorations` derives volumes from unchanged fore outlines and a retained original B1 `storageEnclosure`. It requires exactly one imported shield and a nonempty cut. This saved candidate is paused; integrate it only if Sukhjot later resumes construction; actual publication/reference/walking/full-suite gates remain required. No source/master/public input has changed.

Fixture orchestration writes it without saving/exporting a model. `c01-fore-trim-fit-study.mjs --fixture` independently measures those Blender float-stored triangles against the original exported baseline; it writes `c01-fore-trim-master-check.{json,log}` and rejects any sampled/exact-area intrusion or exterior first-hit difference. The private fixture imports `sunny_exterior` before `sunny_interiors`, matching the existing authoring modules' circular bootstrap order. Its first candidate-import attempt stopped on the reversed import order; it produced no candidate data and no application changes. Independent checks require successful fixture completion first.

Starting from actual source loop triangles still left 2/271/17; `c01-fore-trim-fixture-loop-triangle-failed.{json,log}` preserve that failure. `count_intrusions` now also records remaining sample coordinates and independent mask coverage for diagnosis. The remaining fixture triangles were narrow artifacts along shared triangulation seams (sample vertices only micrometres apart). `prism_masks` and `subtract_convex` now use Python double-precision tuple arithmetic for clipping; `trim_fixture` explicitly fan-triangulates convex retained fragments and rejects zero-area pieces before Blender float storage. No clearance tolerance or room contour has been relaxed. Repeat private-fixture evidence is still required.

The corrected unsaved fixture clears all three independent intrusion samples. `exterior_hits` adds independent BVH geometry/material first hits from688 fixed bow/port/starboard outside rays; orchestration rejects any >1mm/material difference before accepting even private candidate evidence. `c01-fore-trim-fixture-hull.{json,log}` preserve the passing temporary convex-hull storage fixture. The exported-triangle baseline study explicitly retains its earlier disclosed hull approximation.

That stricter native-floor assumption was rejected: the saved B1 floor has already undergone downstream cuts/triangulation. `c01-b1-floor-topology.json` records its exact world vertices/faces for read-only boundary analysis. The published metadata omits authoring `shift`; the fixture derives it from the original B1 level equation and published experience scale before calling `fit_floor`. It does not assume the serialized metadata retains every authoring-only key.

### Verified cannon implementation inventory

`docs/review/cannon-ui-focused-20261022/` preserves the six inspected UI PNGs, `cannon-ui-results.json`, and the browser/logic/build logs from focused verification before the full runner repeats their conventional paths. The exact image paths/hashes are listed in `cannon-ui-verification.json`. These are data/log/images, no functions; source/asset freeze remains intact.

- `src/components/CannonControls.jsx` — global visible Cannon trigger and accessible Open/Close Mouth / Fire Gaon panel. `CannonControls` derives readiness from published navigation state and manages focus; nested `showPanel` releases pointer lock, `operate` invokes the shared real action and publishes immediate feedback/state. An effect focuses the mouth button on open and restores the trigger on close. Escape closes the panel; standard buttons support Space/Enter/Tab. No helm prerequisite, free cola or debug trigger.
- Browser verification caught focus moving to the body when Fire disables after a shot. Nested `closeOnEscape` now registers a cleaned-up capture-phase Escape listener while open, so closure/focus restoration works even then. The earlier failed browser recording is not acceptance evidence. The old stale Vite process was refreshed on the same port; current preview is tool session80806.
- `src/components/UI.jsx:UI` renders the panel after entering the experience and advertises X on the start card/help. Flight collision moves below the left HUD to avoid the Cannon trigger.
- `src/App.jsx:App` guards `onCanvasClick` while the panel is open; `UI` hides capture overlay/flight toggle until closure, keeping the panel usable without accidental pointer re-capture or overlapping controls. Browser regression checks an actual outside-panel canvas click while open.
- `src/state/useStore.js` adds `cannonPanelOpen` and `setCannonPanelOpen` for explicit input handoff.
- `src/hooks/useInput.js:useInput` adds X to release pointer lock/toggle the panel; an effect clears held movement keys when opened; focused ordinary controls/panel input no longer dispatch movement/jump/toggles. Key-up still releases inputs.
- `src/systems/navigation.js` adds `cannonAvailability(state)` for shared cola/Burst/cooldown readiness; `operateCannon(action)` for actual mouth toggle / existing `fireGaon`; `advanceCannon(dt)` for real jaw/blast ticking independent of navigation. `advanceVoyage` reuses that clock. Existing costs/cooldowns/helm functions stay authoritative.
- `src/components/NavigationSystem.jsx:NavigationSystem` routes legacy helm J/K through the shared action; ignores menu-focused navigation keys; ticks the actual cannon while Inspect keeps voyage movement paused. Mesh/collider/effect paths remain unchanged.
- `tests/navigation.test.mjs` retains existing assertions and adds non-helm cannon costs/readiness, Inspect jaw/cooldown ticking without voyage travel and invalid-action guards.
- `tests/cannonControls.mjs` — actual visible-control regression with read-only state/mesh measurements. Nested `start` enters through title; `sample` records actual state/pose/feet/focus. Covers Inspect mouse/Space/Enter/Tab, close/open, real blast/cola, ordinary spawn X pointer-lock exit, no jump/placement/helm, reverse recoil, re-capture and narrow fly viewport. Produces `docs/review/cannon-ui-{inspect-open,inspect-fired,empty,spawn-open,spawn-fired,fly-narrow}.png` and `cannon-ui-results.json`; data/images contain no functions.
- `tests/run-all.mjs` retains all original17 suites and adds the universal cannon browser suite (18 total). Single entry remains `npm test`.

`docs/review/cannon-ui-full-run.mjs` — terminal verification wrapper; `filesAt` enumerates frozen inputs, `manifest` hashes them, `save` persists phase/PIDs/receipts, `run` captures exact npm command exits. Runs the unchanged original17 plus new cannon suite and build without ROOM_FILTER. Outputs `docs/review/cannon-ui-full-*/{status.json,test.log,build.log}`; data/log files contain no functions. `docs/review/cannon-ui-{browser,logic,build}.log`, `cannon-ui-verification.json` preserve focused PASS outcomes and six personally inspected images. The cannon aggregate is terminal18/18 + build PASS; current exterior verification is separate.

No environment variables, original FBX, master or generated models change in UI01. The cannon milestone is terminal verified done; current exterior verification is separate.

Sukhjot has authorized repairing the audit findings sequentially and requested an early universal visible cannon control. `docs/whole-ship-blueprint-audit.md` is the authoritative progress/resume file: all59 original finding IDs, new UI01, U decisions, active/next task, evidence and journal. Original audit/survey-only statements below are historical. `docs/to-do.md` and `docs/section-progress.md` now explicitly defer to that queue; their old status text is historical. These Markdown files contain no functions. No new environment variables.

## October 22 — whole-ship survey (documentation only)

The active request in the fresh survey chat is an audit of the existing published build, not continuation of repairs. The historical repair instructions/status below do not authorize implementation in this survey. The audit preserves source, tests, original FBX, master and published assets; it uses the existing port 5173 preview and distinct `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-*` evidence names. The pre-existing full runner is preserved. A passing route/structure test is not visual reference acceptance. No environment variables are added or changed.

Audit evidence inventory (JSON, CSV and images contain no functions):
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-baseline.json` — SHA-256/size/mtime baseline of implementation, tests, references, original/master and published assets.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-meshes.json` — selected exported mechanism/room mesh names, exact bounds, tags and material inventory.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-room-*.png` (24 views) and `whole-ship-audit-room-views.json` — current rendered room appearance, exact camera/first-hit metadata and framing limitations, produced by the existing `tools/interior_audit.mjs`.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-exterior-{top,port,starboard,front,stern,bow-quarter,stern-quarter,lookout,garden}.png`, `whole-ship-audit-exterior-exterior-inventory.{json,csv}`, `whole-ship-audit-exterior-appearance-context.json` — nine current exterior appearance views and all 7,549 exported mesh bounds/tags. The top view explicitly hides sails/flags/rigging; other views retain them. Produced by the existing `tools/exterior_audit.mjs`.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-contact-room-{1,2,3,4,5,6}.jpg` and `whole-ship-audit-contact-exterior-{1,2,3}.jpg` — labeled inspection sheets of those original captures; source originals retained.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-capture.mjs` — evidence-only browser survey against current port 5173 assets. `three` converts coordinates; `pause` yields between observations; browser `followShipForSurvey` follows the real ship matrix; `view` records an appearance pose, first hits/state and PNG; `aim` changes look orientation only; `action` dispatches real keys and records visible prompts/state; `install` reinstalls the survey render camera; `save` serializes functional traces; `auditNoCameraOverride` retains a valid no-op render callback. Top-level orchestration captures 49 detailed poses and an optional actual WASD-to-helm/control survey without player placement, regeneration or implementation changes.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-title.png`, the detailed pose PNGs named in `whole-ship-audit-supplemental-views.json`, and `whole-ship-audit-contact-detail-{01..13}.jpg` — supplementary room, closet, deck, mechanism and six-vehicle views. The first four closet cameras from the base room utility miss their fixtures; these detailed views replace their appearance coverage.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-verified-{control-*.png,mouth-open-close.png,gaon-fire-close.png,chicken-close.png,dock-open-port.png,functional.json,views.json}` / `whole-ship-audit-capture.log` — corrected control recording and remote mechanism-camera metadata. First recording files are retained under `whole-ship-audit-rejected-*`: the evidence helper used a null scene callback, causing rendering errors; they are not application defects or accepted control-image evidence.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-static.mjs` — evidence-only exported geometry inspection. `visit` collects exact transformed GLB triangles/materials; `hits` independently intersects selected visual surfaces; `probeCannon` traces each closet cannon's outward muzzle centerline. Top-level orchestration records the food-lift vertical obstruction, candidate hatch rail bounds, and lookout fixture inventory. `whole-ship-audit-static.{json,log}` contain the resulting data, with AABB candidates distinguished from triangle proof.

No new file, function, environment variable, test or rebuild was introduced.

- `docs/whole-ship-blueprint-audit.md` — comprehensive current-publication report: all 25 firsthand references, 27 metadata spaces plus lookout / 19 accuracy groups, exterior coverage, unique construction/room/control/graphics findings, explicit variants/limits and later repair order. Markdown, no functions.

- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-orientation.json` — bow-up blueprint / world-axis mapping with actual slide/swing, Usopp workbench/sign and Franky furnace centroids; no functions.

Camera-only evidence; no functions. The capture script supports controls-only/extra-only/curtain-only evidence runs.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-inherited-test-{snapshot.log,status.json}` — read-only cutoff copy of the pre-existing suite; all 27 room-entry checks logged, aggregate runner still active without final summary. No functions.
- `docs/review/whole-ship-audit-evidence.json` — exact image/reference hashes and inspection dispositions, current served-vs-disk asset hashes, zero unexpected implementation input changes, accepted functional trace and pre-existing-run cutoff. No functions. The current evidence includes 107 non-rejected-control PNG captures (framing limitations disclosed), 28 personally inspected contact sheets, 25 personally inspected reference images and 10 rejected original control PNGs.

The completed survey changes documentation/evidence only. Original/master/public assets, runtime, authoring pipeline, tests and configuration remain unchanged from the survey baseline. No assets/server/full suite/production build were started or regenerated by this survey. No new environment variables. Thousand Sunny — interactive 3D experience. A React Three Fiber app that renders a
Blender-processed version of `stuff/1.FBX` and lets the player walk, fly and orbit it
on the open sea.

This file is the authoritative inventory of every file in the project and the functions
it contains. Update it whenever anything changes.

## Layout

```
.
├── index.html                 Vite entry document
├── vite.config.js             dev server + build config
├── package.json               deps and scripts
├── plan.md                    goals, decisions, checklist, build log
├── readme.md                  original project brief (do not edit)
├── stuff/1.FBX                SOURCE ASSET — read only, never modified
├── tools/build_assets.py      Blender asset pipeline (clean → scale → tag → collide → export)
├── assets/sunny-master.blend  generated editable Blender master, separate from web assets
├── public/
│   ├── favicon.svg
│   └── models/                generated assets (never hand-edited)
│       ├── sunny.glb              restored visual ship; counts in metadata
│       ├── sunny-collision.glb    layered floor/obstacle collision proxy
│       └── ship-metadata.json     hull/deck/spawn/parts/water for the app
├── src/                       application source
└── tests/                     test suites (single runner: tests/run-all.mjs)
```

## Data flow

1. `tools/build_assets.py` imports `stuff/1.FBX` in Blender, drops junk, normalizes a
   39 m reference envelope, restores geometry/materials/deck features, extends the
   elevated rig to 56 m, applies the configured experience scale, tags every mesh,
   raycasts layered walking surfaces, and writes `public/models/*`.
2. At runtime `src/App.jsx` loads `sunny.glb` + `ship-metadata.json`.
3. `src/systems/shipMeta.js` converts Blender Z-up metadata to three.js Y-up.
4. `src/components/Ship.jsx` clones the GLB and assigns authored base-colour PBR
   materials per tag. Selected final-UV exterior surfaces receive filtered PBR
   maps; furnished room furniture retains its base material until its phase.
5. `src/systems/collision.js` loads `sunny-collision.glb` and answers the queries the
   player controller makes each frame.
6. `src/components/PlayerController.jsx` integrates movement, gravity and collision.

Current phase instruction (October 14 continuation): complete exterior structures, then accurate furnished rooms and the main build, then realistic graphics, then additional mechanisms. The active user goal authorizes this whole sequence. Historical construction checkpoints and review holds below describe earlier publications; they are not current acceptance evidence.

October 14 source changes: `tools/sunny_interiors.py::wall_run` accepts optional `front_lintel_key` and assigns that material only to the actual local +Y outside face. Aquarium, Dining and Kitchen entrance lintels use the reference cream facade; their other faces remain timber and their physical dimensions and openings are preserved. `tests/exterior.test.mjs::visit` collects actual entrance lintel triangle normals/materials; the new final group proves bounded cream outside faces and retained interior timber on all three entrances. All original 32 geometry groups remain. New publication and exported-face verification are pending. `src/components/PlayerController.jsx` exposes `__sunny.nearbyColliders`, a read-only getter that serializes nearby actual runtime static and dynamic bounds for movement diagnosis; it does not alter player physics.

## Files and functions

### `tools/interior_audit.mjs`
Matched human-eye interior screenshot capture, expressly separate from physical journey proof.
- `room(name)` / `centre(name)` — reference room metadata lookup and centre measurements.
- `toThree(p)` — converts Blender coordinates for the inspection camera.
- Top-level orchestration captures twenty-four current room views and renderer/errors/eye metadata with one browser. No room is teleported or walked for acceptance; the file reports appearance only.

### `tools/sunny_room_details.py`
Auxiliary source-supported room completion in independent human metres.
- `aquarium_drapery` — supports side curtains and eight tank-rail-following cloth swags.
- `bath_wc` — a separate arched toilet compartment beyond the preserved stair shaft, with a real door, relocated toilet and small bookshelf; registers a new room/opening.
- `workshop_metals` — Franky's metal lining and correct shared-wall face, retaining Usopp's timber side.
- `lumber_storage` — supported raw timber stock in the forward store.
- `complete_reference_details` — coordinates those source-supported details and re-snapshots opening metadata.
`complete_room_fittings` calls this coordinator before floor patterns and lamps. Its lower-floor material region now distinguishes Franky's metal room. `build_interiors` changes the provisional four-poster wall with Nami to five male wanted portraits (Luffy, Zoro, Usopp, Chopper, Franky); `tools/sunny_details.py::wall_art` adds supported Chopper/Franky identity cues. Fine portrait artwork remains graphics work.

### `tools/sunny_room_accuracy.py`
Authoring for the supplied Volume 46 SBS/anime room references, in independent human metres. Integrated after exterior mechanisms and before final surface preparation; current room publication is pending. `tools/sunny_interiors.py::build_interiors` corrects the F2 programme to port Dining/Sick Bay and starboard Kitchen/Pantry, including entrances, furniture, drawer-facing rotations, door labels and metadata. `tests/exterior.test.mjs` retains the exact physical stations/width/headroom checks with their corrected F2 room names on the source-supported opposite sides. `cannon` now builds a closed connected hollow barrel with a real blind bore, an aligned muzzle rim, a solid carriage and four round wheels/hubs. Its direction/bore tags remain inspectable in the master and GLB. `Quarters/Womens_cannon` point outboard and closet side names follow the documented Blender -Y port convention. These source edits await the room publication. `tools/sunny_exterior.py::restore_exterior` calls the new room coordinator before final surface preparation. `tools/build_assets.py` exports supported room light records, saves a compressed editable `assets/sunny-master.blend` with Exterior/Interior/Interactive collections before GLB export, and publishes the master only after successful asset generation. Collection organization preserves object transforms and GLB nodes. `tests/assets.test.mjs` keeps its shared-span assertion with the corrected side order and adds direct canonical side/entrance, fixture, three-barrel capacity, lamp metadata and editable-master checks.
- `soft_box` — closed furniture panels with rounded edges and controlled face shading.
- `remove_family` — replaces a named provisional furnishing without removing the ship shell.
- `repaint_family` — reference upholstery and metal base colours.
- `tile_floor` — subdivides the actual existing floor top into checkerboard regions, retaining hatches, geometry height and a single surface owner; nested `emit` removes repeated/zero-area clipping vertices and appends material-labelled polygons.
- `cloth_panel` — closed folded fabric with supported header rail.
- `pendant` — supported caged marine fixture and a later runtime light record.
- `rounded_table` — the library's octagonal central table and pedestal.
- `stocked_shelf` — pantry shelves, jars and raw produce.
- `library_details` — green perimeter seating, aft survey desk and central table.
- `quarters_details` — laundry, actual-ceiling bunk suspensions and women's bed canopy/standing lamp. Beam translation is measured from real world-space bounds because shaft cuts may already have baked local geometry.
- `kitchen_details` — giant oven, hood, plate rack, tablecloth and locked fridge cues.
- `infirmary_details` — Chopper's bed/chair, bulletin board and medicine props.
- `workshop_details` — Usopp's factory sign, Franky's furnace and scrap station.
- `arched_chamber` — closed annular boiler housing with a real barrel-loading cavity, a solid back and a supporting floor; surface collision preserves the opening.
- `energy_details` — replaces the provisional five tanks with the SBS three-barrel cola chamber/grille.
- `bath_details` — showers, wash bowls, towels, mirror and ventilation.
- `complete_room_fittings` — coordinates source-led furnishing, floor regions and supported lamp metadata. It preserves original retained mesh identities plus actual additions, excluding helper data from the published object set. It leaves texture and illumination work to the subsequent phase.

October 14 floor ownership correction: `tools/sunny_exterior.py::plan_intersection_area(points, outline)` computes actual clipped XY overlap, with an early bounds rejection. `subtract_plan_polygon` retains untouched/touching polygons rather than repeatedly subdividing them into zero-area fragments. The dense checker-floor fixture reproduced 21,125 baseline fragments in fewer than thirty masks; an actual-intersection guard completes all 450 masks with 1,081 pieces in 0.309 s. This is a geometry-preserving performance repair, not a reduction of ship/room scope or test coverage. `restore_exterior` now flushes phase messages so live build work is observable. Room build 45868 was explicitly terminated after this diagnosed fragmentation; its published assets remained unchanged. The replacement room build must pass all original/current geometry and access guards.

October 14 room-access correction: `src/systems/doors.js::stepClear` uses the complete endpoint-union sweep for all translating leaves, retaining the existing flat-hatch resting-foot rule. Ordinary walking contact with vertical doors now keeps 21 mm spacing matching the existing 20 mm animation safety band; `src/systems/collision.js::resolveList` is exported for direct testing and `blockedIn` uses the same contact spacing. Player radius/height and published physical leaf dimensions are unchanged. `tests/doors.test.mjs` directly joins the actual walking resolver and door state machine for both wall orientations and both approach sides; existing hinge/hatch trap and visual/collision checks remain.

### `src/systems/surfaceCollision.js`
Physical section boxes for moving authored triangle surfaces in ship-local Y-up metres.
- `component(point, axis)` — reads a point coordinate.
- `clip(points, axis, edge, above)` — clips a convex polygon to a coordinate half-plane.
- `surfaceCollisionBounds(triangles, spacing, skin)` — validates and clips actual triangles into small horizontal cells with merged physical skin bounds; avoids the full-envelope rudder box spanning the Energy Room.

`src/components/NavigationSystem.jsx::rudderSurfaceSections(node, inverse)` extracts actual mesh triangles in ship-local coordinates and caches section corners once per loaded ship. The rudder branch of `NavigationSystem` publishes each section under the same steering pivot rotation as the visible blade. `movingBounds` accepts either a mechanism node or a named surface-section record, preserving distinct collider identities. All genuine blade surfaces remain solid; other mechanisms retain their bounds. `tests/physics.test.mjs` adds real-surface coverage, empty-envelope clearance, finite thickness and malformed-input checks through the existing single runner. This repairs a reproduced Energy Room collision with `Line012` before the room build phase. `tests/gameplay.mjs` now approaches the actual B1 dock/workshop door instead of placing the player inside the occupied Kurosai bay: its original >5 m / grounded assertions remain and an explicit real-door crossing assertion is added.


### `src/config/renderConfig.js`
Finite ocean budget: full-window canvas, DPR 1, 220 m square ocean with
96 segments per axis and 0.2 wave displacement scale. An 1800 m horizon ring
adds only eight flat triangles; the detailed plane remains exactly 96 × 96 segments; the near waves fade smoothly into it. No functions.
`index.html` sizes the frame to the full window; UI overlays are absolute
inside the frame. `App` keeps the human and ship scale independent of render size.
`Environment` uses a 450 m sky and 1024 px sunlight shadow map.
`Ocean` receives the converted 32-station hull waterline profile, discards interior
water fragments, uses a waterline-relative color blend and small animated ripples
for reflected highlights. Its vertex displacement and `waveHeightAt` share WAVE_SCALE.
Fragments outside the hull's X extent skip the cutout loop; matching intervals exit early.
`useEffect` exposes `__sunnyOcean` for tests and disposes replaced materials.
`convertMeta` maps `oceanHullProfile` from Blender lateral bounds to Three X/Z bounds.
- `surfaceNoiseTexture()` in `materials.js` — one repeatable 256 px micro-detail
  texture shared by all PBR surfaces. Local `hash(x,y)` seeds its noise.
- `authoredMaterial(source,{solid,vertical})` clones/caches stable exported base
  colours with per-family roughness, gold reflections and translucent glass.
  Fine material textures remain paused for construction QA. The old world-space
  grain/relief injection has been removed; legacy fallback helpers remain.
- `hull_sides(objects,x,z)` in `sunny_exterior.py` — measures inner side clearances.
- `add_interior_shell(objects)` — hull-fitted lower floor/linings and bare bulkheads
  with open door passages; reserves B1 storage/dock/workshop, F1 fore quarters/aft aquarium, F2 fore quarters
  and dining/kitchen/pantry/sick bay, and the F3 circular library floor.
  Furnished rooms remain deferred. Aquarium reserve dimensions are recorded in metadata.
- `waterline_profile(objects,height)` in `build_assets.py` — mesh/plane intersections
  sampled into 32 lateral cross-section stations for the ocean cutout.
  Metadata records dry shell floor levels, partition count and unfinished room status.

### `index.html`
Vite entry document. Mount point `#root`, loads `/src/main.jsx`, page title and favicon.

### `vite.config.js`
Vite configuration. React plugin, dev server on port 5173.

### `src/main.jsx`
React entry point. Creates the root and renders `<App/>` with `StrictMode`.

### `src/App.jsx`
Top-level component: canvas, loading screen, scene assembly, mode wiring.
- `ProgressBridge()` — subscribes to `useProgress` and pushes load percentage into the store.
- `SceneRig({ meta, keys, look })` — builds the scene graph: environment, procedural env
  map, ocean, ship, collision, player controller, HUD messages.
- `App()` (default export) — converts metadata to three.js space, owns the
  Inspect ↔ Explore switch and the title-screen gate.

### `src/config/shipConfig.js`
Central tuning constants (no functions).
`SHIP_LENGTH_M`, `PLAYER_HEIGHT_M`, `EYE_HEIGHT_M`, `WALK_SPEED`, `RUN_SPEED`,
`JUMP_SPEED`, `GRAVITY`, `GROUND_ACCEL`, `AIR_ACCEL`, `FLY_SPEED`, `FLY_FAST_SPEED`,
`MOUSE_SENSITIVITY`, `THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE`, `THIRD_PERSON_HEIGHT`, `WATER_LEVEL`,
`WAVE_HEIGHT`, `WAVE_LENGTH`, `WIND`, `CAMERA_MODES`, `PERSON_MODES`, `MOVEMENT_MODES`,
`SHIP_COLORS`.
`EXPERIENCE_SCALE`, `SHIP_LENGTH_M` and `SHIP_HEIGHT_M` read the shared exterior
configuration. Life-size eye height is 1.7 m.

### `src/config/exteriorConfig.json`
Shared Python/runtime settings: reference length 39 m, reference height 56 m,
and user-authorized uniform experience scale 2.2. No functions. Configured ship
length is 85.8 m and height 123.2 m; the earlier lawn expansion is retained. Rebuild
assets after changing this file; the player dimensions and speeds remain human-sized.

### `src/state/useStore.js`
Zustand store shared by the whole app (no functions of its own beyond the state
setters it defines): `started`, `locked`, `loading`, `progress`, `cameraMode`,
`personMode`, `movementMode`, `flying`, `flyCollision` (default false), `position`, `yaw`, `pitch`, `speed`, `grounded`,
`windStrength`, `windDirection`, `showHelp`, `hudMessage`, plus `setLoading`,
`setStarted`, `setLocked`, `toggleCameraMode`, `togglePersonMode`, `setMovementMode`,
`toggleFlight`, `toggleFlyCollision`, `setPlayer`, `setWind`, `toggleHelp`, `setHudMessage`.

### `src/hooks/useInput.js`
- `useInput()` — keyboard, mouse-look and pointer-lock handling. Returns the `keys` map
  (WASD, Shift, Space, `E`/`Q` for fly altitude, `V`/`C`/`F`/`H` mode keys, `B` flight collision), the `look`
  delta accumulator, and the pointer-lock request/release helpers.

### `src/systems/shipMeta.js`
Metadata loading and axis conversion.
- `useShipMeta()` — fetches and caches `ship-metadata.json`, returns converted meta.
- `toThree(x, y, z)` — Blender Z-up → three.js Y-up point conversion.
- `boxToThree(min, max)` — same conversion for an axis-aligned box.
- `convertMeta(raw)` — converts hull, deck, spawn, parts and collision sections.
  Reorders hull size as well as min/max to Three's Y-up axes for accurate framing.
- `fallbackMeta()` — conservative defaults if the fetch fails, so the app still boots.

### `src/systems/collision.js`
- `useCollision()` — loads `sunny-collision.glb`, flattens every mesh into an
  axis-aligned `Box3` and exposes the queries below.
  - `world.boxes` / `world.floors` — all colliders, and the floor subset.
  - `raycastDown(origin, maxDist, radius)` — highest floor top under the requested
    footprint. Walking and the void guard use the player radius so stair corner
    support is retained after a step; point probes remain available for diagnostics.
  - `stepHeightAt(position, radius, maxRise)` — footprint query for low edges,
    including diagonal corners missed by the centre ray; controller checks full
    body clearance before stepping and retains horizontal momentum.
  - `resolve(position, velocity, radius, height)` — axis-separated capsule-vs-world
    resolution so the player slides along walls; returns the pushed-out position,
    the zeroed velocity and the blocking collider name.
  - `moveFlight(position,velocity,dt,radius,height)` — shared swept flight solver.
  - `hasCeiling(position, radius, fromY, height)` — detects overhead solid surfaces.
  - `isBlocked(position, radius, height)` — checks the full standing body clearance.

### `src/systems/flightCollision.js`
- `sweepFlight(boxes,position,velocity,dt,radius,height)` — swept axis movement
  against actual solid colliders, including walls/floors/ceilings. Prevents boosted
  tunnelling; excludes artificial walking boundary guards and the safety catch.

### `src/systems/stepSupport.js`
- `stepSupport(boxes, position, radius, maxRise)` — highest climbable floor top
  intersecting the collision footprint. Excludes distant surfaces, overhead decks
  and heights beyond the allowed step. Shared by runtime and physics regression tests.

### `src/systems/materials.js`
Stable authored base-colour materials; legacy procedural materials remain for the deferred texture phase.
- `base(name, params)` — shared `MeshStandardMaterial` factory.
- `woodMaterial({ color, dark, repeat, plankScale })` — procedural plank shader.
- `shipSurfaceMaterial({ deckY, waterY })` — single material that blends hull and deck
  per fragment by world normal and waterline, so the two large merged meshes read
  correctly.
- `deckWoodMaterial({ color, dark, plankWidth, rowLength })` — deck planking with a warm
  finish.
- `hullMaterial({ color, seam, plankScale })` — painted hull with seam lines.
- `sailMaterial({ color, stripe, stripeAt })` — canvas with an optional colour band.
- `metalMaterial({ color, roughness, metalness })` — fittings and hardware.
- `ropeMaterial({ color })` — rigging.
- `fallbackMaterial()` — used for any untagged mesh.
- `resolveMaterial(part, name, deckY, waterY)` — maps a semantic tag from the pipeline
  (`sail`, `mast`, `rigging`, `trim`, `deck`, `hull`) to a material.
- `authoredMaterial(source)` — preserves exported per-face base paint and calibrated
  roughness/metalness without procedural grain or relief during geometry QA.
  Two-sided surfaces show the structural shell. Memoized by exported material name.
  Legacy options are retained for later texture work. Ocean normal/foam scaling
  remains tied to the actual gentle wave displacement.

### `src/systems/sails.js`
Shared wind state and the animated sail material.
- `makeSailMaterial({...})` — sail material whose vertex shader billows with the wind.
  Authored UVs select plain canvas, the source-led vector Straw Hat Jolly Roger,
  red/black aft panels and black pirate flags. Ship-local projection remains the
  fallback for source UVs; all four canvas edges and each flag pole edge are pinned.
- `emblemTexture(texture)` — configures cached artwork filtering, mipmaps, colour
  space and anisotropy without initiating a loader during component rendering.
- `windState` — live wind strength/direction shared by every sail.
- `updateWind(t)` — advances the wind so all sails breathe together.

### `src/components/Ship.jsx`
- `Ship({ sunDir, skyColor, deckY, waterY })` — clones the visual GLB, applies
  `resolveMaterial` per mesh, drives the shared wind, bobs the ship and renders the
  animated sails.
  Reads tags from multi-material parent groups as well as mesh nodes; preserves
  authored paint and assigns distinct sail/flag designs. Stops visual bob in Explore
  so visible deck surfaces stay aligned with the static collision proxy.
- `inferPart(name)` — last-resort tag inference for meshes with no custom property.

### `src/components/Environment.jsx`
- `Lighting()` — key/fill/rim lights tuned for a bright sea day.
- `SkyDome()` — gradient sky dome.
- `InspectCamera({ target, distance })` — `OrbitControls` used in Inspect mode.

### `src/components/EnvironmentMap.jsx`
- `ProceduralEnvironment({ sunDir, resolution })` — renders a small procedural sky into a
  PMREM cube so PBR materials get image-based lighting without any texture files.

### `src/components/Ocean.jsx`
Gerstner-wave ocean plane with fresnel, sun specular and foam.
- `horizonGeometry()` — four flat rectangles around the near ocean, eight triangles,
  extending the visible sea without distant wave tessellation.
- `Ocean({waterLevel,hullProfile,...props},ref)` — finite square mesh; memoizes the
  hull-masked shader, updates wave/time/camera uniforms and exposes a test hook.
  A shared shader/clock shades near and far water; edge weight fades near waves
  and normals into the flat horizon. The debug hook also exposes horizon geometry.
  GLSL `gerstner` sums displacement/tangents; `hash` and `noise` break up ripples/foam.
- `waveHeightAt(x, z, time)` — analytic wave height, used to keep the ship and the
  player consistent with the visible surface.

### `src/components/PlayerController.jsx`
- `PlayerController({ keys, look, spawn, waterLevel, onFrame })` — the whole movement
  simulation: walk/run acceleration, gravity, jumping, ground snapping, step-up,
  wall sliding, ceiling checks, fall/void guard with respawn, fly mode, third-person
  camera, and the `window.__sunny` debug hook used by the browser tests.
  Flight bypasses collision by default; with the toggle on, uses swept 3-axis collision.
  Walking collision is always on. Debug hook exposes flight collision state.
  New arrivals face the bow (+π/2 yaw) from the lawn instead of the nearby aft wall.

### `src/components/UI.jsx`
- `UI({ onEnter })` — title screen, control legend and the in-world HUD.
  Flight shows an accessible ON/OFF button (`aria-pressed`), also toggled with B.
  The button stays above the mouse capture overlay so Esc makes it clickable.

### `tools/build_assets.py`
The Blender pipeline. Functions:
- `log(*a)` — flushed pipeline logging.
- `_safe_read_geom(fbx_tmpl, fbx_obj, settings)` — imports the FBX while skipping the one
  degenerate mesh that crashes Blender's importer.
- `vertex_bbox(o)` — true world-space bounds from the mesh vertices (the FBX's reported
  bounds under-report badly).
- `is_junk(name)` — matches the known junk objects.
- `world_bbox(objects)` — aggregate true bounds.
- `guess_look(name)` — rough surface classification from naming.
- `obb(o)` — memoized true world vertex bounds after restoration; cached imported
  `bound_box` extents are unsuitable for collision and previously invented obstacles.
- `walkable_hit(x, y)` — raycast down through the sails to find deck surfaces.
- `region_stats(r)` — per-deck-region statistics.
- `classify(o, a, b)` — assigns a semantic part tag to a mesh.
- `add_mesh(name, verts, faces)` / `add_box(name, loc, scale)` — collision mesh builders.
- `clip_polygon(points, axis, edge, above)` — clips a polygon to an axis plane.
- `face_sections(points)` — slices diagonal railing faces into 15 cm sections so
  collider bounds stay close to the actual surface, including the curved port flight.
- `wx(i)` / `wy(j)` — cell-origin helpers for the collision grid.
- `curved_floor_boxes(obj, spacing, thickness)` — clips actual standable curved-bed triangles into small support footprints and fits each proxy from the lowest structural underside to the highest actual bed point. Used for the starboard chute with 10 cm maximum footprints/6 cm structure, preserving support before its fitted solid posts. Excludes duplicate coarse chute cells while retaining other floor layers/terrain planning.
- `export_glb(path, objects)` — glTF export wrapper.
- `exported_translations(path)` — reads node translations back out of an exported GLB.
- `blender_to_gltf(v)` — Z-up to Y-up vector conversion.
- Imports `restore_exterior` after linked-object repair, before raycasts: restores
  authored materials, outdoor details, visible stairways and the 56 m rig height.
  Generated metadata records the shared height offset, references and feature names.
  Raycasts pass through decorative foliage and fittings, sampling only authored
  walking surfaces; authored obstacle flags determine which detail boxes are solid.
  Obstacle tops are capped at the actual source mesh height, preventing short
  stair treads from becoming 2.4 m invisible walls. Both masts and the circular
  library cabin use full-height surface colliders for collision-enabled flight.
  Hanging `NGon*` lantern fittings are excluded from obstacle boxes because their
  imported bounds extend below visible geometry and block the balcony beneath them.
  Source fore/aft stair treads and authored garden treads use thin exact
  `COLL_FloorStair_*` boxes instead of aliased 40 cm terrain samples. Their floor
  columns suppress containment walls, and treads never become obstacle boxes.
  Tube/lantern and fore stair railing tops are excluded from floor raycasts.
  Curved `Box080..083` rails and `Cylinder015` use individual vertical-face
  `COLL_Rail_*` boxes, preserving open passages instead of filling their full bounds.
  Decorative side-scroll tops (`Line004`/`Line007`) are also excluded from floors.
  Floor slabs are 0.6 m thick so upper decks do not create 3 m solid volumes below
  them. Authored floor layers retain landings and room floors below
  upper surfaces; all coplanar samples are merged into `COLL_Floor_*` rectangles.
  Collision metadata reports sample counts, merged boxes and floor thickness.
  Authored floor raycasts run before proxy meshes are created to avoid repeatedly
  invalidating Blender's evaluation cache. Logging flushes for live build progress.
- `merge_floor_cells(cells)` — merges equal-height/depth floor samples into exact
  rectangles without filling holes or joining different levels. Greatly reduces
  runtime collision boxes and GLB loading while retaining the sampled walking outline.
  Metadata records sample counts and actual floorColliderBoxes separately.
  Layered room-floor and forward upper-deck ceiling collision slabs are 18 cm thick to preserve doorway/ceiling clearance.
  Source cabin walls, transverse fore guards and aft stair rails also use sectioned face collision.
  Authored solid partitions use full-height `COLL_Solid_*` boxes independently of
  top-down terrain hits, so lower-level walls cannot disappear beneath upper floors.
  Boundary walls respect authored floor columns so an old deck edge cannot block
  entry onto a new landing or exterior balcony. Balcony floor winding faces upward.

### `tools/sunny_exterior.py`
Reference-led exterior restoration in original imported metre coordinates.
- `material(key)` — shared palette, sRGB to linear export colors.
- `finish(obj, name, key, walkable, obstacle)` — authored detail tags and materials.
- `box(...)`, `sphere(...)`, `pole(...)`, `ring(...)` — geometric building blocks.
- `text(...)` — short markings converted into mesh geometry.
- `tree(...)` — branched leafy shade/citrus trees and planter pots.
- `stairs(...)` — visible treads and rails, with exact thin tread floor collision.
- `paint_source(objects)` — explicit per-face colors on audited source parts.
  White scroll rims surround red side infills; the bow lion mount has a black
  center and gold rim/bolts. Face features use the measured Blender lateral axis.
- `add_exterior(fore_z,dock_sides)` — lawn, gardens, helm/navigation controls, slide, swing,
  hatches, glazing, deck stairs, lion eyes and exterior machinery fittings.
  Soldier Dock plates measure both hull attachments, replace old duplicate numerals,
  and use white paint with red channel numbers and black rims.
  Garden upper landings begin at the last tread instead of bridging above five
  treads, so descending has no abrupt hidden drop. Planters/chimney bases sit on the deck.
  Garden stairs are inset into roof access slots, inside the hull, with inward exit landings; a
  walkable annular stern balcony surrounds the circular room at garden height.
  Decorations are excluded from
  floor raycasts; rope ladders are excluded from floor sampling as well as sail animation.
  Retains original fore/aft stair flights instead of adding duplicate corner stairs
  or a second bow flight beyond the real foredeck.
- `restore_exterior(objects, vertex_bbox)` — adds exterior then extends the elevated
  rig to 56 m; preserves human-scale deck structures and translates the waterline.
  Expands the lawn from 7.1 to 10.65 m longitudinally inside the 39 m envelope;
  shifts fittings/trees as rigid feature clusters and preserves mast diameter.
  Finally enlarges the ship by the shared experience scale. Helm/controls and exterior
  doors keep human dimensions around their enlarged mounting positions.
  Collision is generated after this transform; waterline/spawn/landmarks follow it.
  Moves the port lower stair treads outward by 0.45 m before enlargement, clearing
  the round cabin wall. Aligns both fore lower flights to the shared middle-deck height, removes
  central upper treads buried beneath that platform, and widens original flights;
  opens the integrated `Line006` stair guards and adds a broad upper exit landing with a side gate onto the forward deck.
  Lower rails stop before the transfer, and upper rails begin beyond its first treads; replaces `Box080`–`Box083` rails.
  Opens `Box070`/`Box084` at the shared fore cross-landing, removes side panels/windows obstructing those openings, and cuts dining roof
  slots for inset garden stairs.
- `dock_attachment_sides(objects,x,z)` — outer painted-rim positions near each
  Soldier Dock opening, avoiding plates buried behind the red side panels.
- `fitted_floor(objects,name,xlo,xhi,z)` — hull-fitted room floor outlines.
- `bulkhead(name,x,lo,hi,z,height,door_y)` — bare partitions with human-sized openings.
- `cap_roof(obj,height)` — trims the warped roof above the garden floor while retaining cabin walls.
- `plan_floor(name,outline,z)` — upward polygonal structural floor.
- `add_garden_deck()` — level rounded F3 garden platform, with stair access slots.
- `clip_face(points,axis,edge,above)` — polygon half-plane clipping.
- `open_plan_slot(obj,xlo,xhi,ylo,yhi)` — material-preserving access openings.
- `rebuild_stair_rails(objects,vertex_bbox)` — rebuilds white rails/posts following
  both fore lower flights and the widened central upper flight; tagged for precise per-face rail collision.
- `layout_x(x)` — piecewise longitudinal redistribution; central band expands
  while fore/aft structure shorten slightly and overall endpoints stay fixed.
- `repair_symmetry(objects)` — reconstructs three displaced source side pieces
  from mirrored world-space geometry with corrected winding; removes original eye
  placeholders before adding face-aligned eyes and a curved muzzle smile.
- `surface_height(objects, x, y, ceiling)` — measures the actual source deck beneath
  the helm. Its returned elevation anchors helm/Log Pose/Soldier Dock and the
  foredeck landmark. The pedestal reaches the wheel hub.

### `docs/exterior-reference.md`
Source links, post-timeskip target, published dimensions and reconstruction limits.
Tracks the supplied four-level plans, intentionally enlarged experience dimensions,
room reserves, stair transfers, solid walls, optional flight collision, low-cost
ocean horizon and validation.
No executable functions.

Scale and centring are done as two separate passes on purpose: scaling first, then
re-measuring and centring, because the first bbox read after import can be stale.
World transforms are also captured before the FBX parent Empties are deleted, because
`bpy.data.objects.remove()` silently drops a deleted parent's contribution from its
children.

### `public/models/ship-metadata.json`
Generated. `interiorShell` records B1/F1/F2/F3 floors, bare partition counts and
reserved aquarium/cabin dimensions, with finishedRooms=false.
`source`, `units`, `axis`, `hull` (min/max/size/center), `waterLevel`,
`deck` (regions), `spawn`, `parts` (tag → mesh names), `collision`, `counts`.
Blender Z-up; the app converts.

## Tests

One entry point runs everything:

```
npm test              # all seventeen suites, serial browser journeys included
npm run test:physics  # offline collision simulation
npm run test:gameplay # drives the real app in headless Chrome
npm run visual        # screenshot capture
```

### `tests/run-all.mjs`
The single test runner. Checks the generated assets exist, starts the dev server if
needed, then runs each suite in order and prints a pass/fail summary.

### `tests/assets.test.mjs`
Structural checks on the generated assets: source FBX untouched, every kept ship mesh
present, named root node, finite vertices, **the GLB's own bounds matching the hull
Blender measured**, the GLB centred on the origin, no stray geometry parked away from
the hull, tags preserved and covering every mesh, collision proxy shape, hull
length/beam agreement with Blender's measured hull, configured proportional scale,
repaired side symmetry, centred eyes, expanded lawn and metadata completeness.

The stray-geometry check is the one that matters most: the glTF exporter silently
mis-places a subset of the FBX's linked objects, which once left the lion figurehead
floating 60 m from the ship.

Additional asset checks cover human-height helm controls, support contact for
trees/planters/bench/instruments, inboard rear treads, symmetric Dock plates and
full-height solid colliders for every bare partition, and full-height surface
collision on both masts and the circular library walls.

### `tests/physics.test.mjs`
Offline simulation of the collision proxy covering gravity, landing, wall containment,
floor integrity and zero-size colliders. Tests the runtime step query on a diagonal
low edge, with overhead, distance and maximum-height rejection.

Swept-flight cases cover boosted thin-wall tunnelling, wall sliding, floor/ceiling
contact and exclusion of artificial walking guards.

### `tests/reachability.mjs`
Flood-fills a layered collision grid from spawn, preserving stairs below balconies.
- `toThree(x,y,z)` — metadata coordinate conversion.
- `colKey(i,j)` — horizontal grid-column key.
- `blocker(node)` — capsule overlap against walls and actual-height obstacles.
Reports accessible floor layers and blocking colliders. `STEP_HEIGHT` matches the
controller. Grid origin is derived from the exported floors instead of assuming zero.
Reach bounds use iterative reductions, avoiding JavaScript argument limits on enlarged
multilevel floor plans.
Rasterizes floor footprints at 10 cm so the source's narrow stair treads retain
connected nodes; it checks route connectivity, while gameplay tests verify climbing.
Obstacle checks use footprint tread support, matching the controller's stair stance.
The graph bridges one empty 10 cm sample between thin adjacent treads, within the
player's support footprint.
Asserts a connected floor route near every recorded exterior landmark: lawn,
forward helm deck, aft dining deck, upper garden and stern balcony.

### `tests/gameplay.mjs`
Drives the real app in headless Chrome — 32 assertions covering walking, running,
jumping, falling and respawning, going over the edge (must be a short recovery, not a
dive to the waterline), camera modes, fly mode, canvas rendering and frame rate.
Reads the generated waterline rather than asserting against a fixed old -14 m datum.
Tests flight default-off, clickable toggle and B shortcut; flies through/stops at
a real partition and confirms walking always stops there.
Also walks all seven real fore/aft/garden stair flights and four complete spawn-to-helm/garden
journeys on both sides; checks landing stability,
upper-floor arrival, bare lower-floor support, an open partition doorway, the
restored full-window view, finite ocean geometry, hull cutout uniforms and wave clearance.

### `tests/stairFlights.mjs`
- `stairFlights(meshes)` — identifies all seven original/authored tread groups from
  exported bounds and returns ascending center paths.
- `walkFlight(page,flight,{teleport,descending})` — enters from the lower landing and follows each flight
  with real movement controls; returns approach stability and climb results; full journeys disable teleport and also descend each flight.
- `driveTo(page,target,tolerance)` — controller-driven waypoint movement with stall detection
  and shorter sampling intervals close to the destination for narrow passage alignment.
- `walkJourney(page,meshes,kind,side)` — spawn-to-helm/garden walks over both sides,
  including lower approaches, cross landings, inward exits and a complete return
  to the lawn by descending both flights, without intermediate teleport. The helm
  approach aligns between the solid fore structure and the Soldier Dock console,
  with tighter arrival tolerance at that narrow passage.
- `sleep(ms)` — bounded sampling delay for the browser controller.

### `tests/interaction.mjs`
Regression test for the "stuck on Click to Orbit" bug. Asserts that in Inspect mode
the canvas is the topmost element everywhere you could drag, that dragging actually
orbits the camera, and that `V` still leaves Inspect mode.

### `tests/smoke.mjs`
Minimal WebGL boot check.

### `tests/visual.mjs`
Captures the review screenshots into `tests/screenshots/` (title, inspect orbit, deck
walk in four directions, deck up, third person, flying).

### `tests/glb.mjs`
Test-only glTF reader used by the suites above.
- `readGLB(path)` — parse the GLB JSON and BIN chunks.
- `readAccessor(gltf, index)` — read any accessor component type, honouring stride and
  normalisation.
- `nodeMatrix(node)` — compose a node's local matrix from `matrix` or TRS.
- `loadColliders(path)` — every mesh in a GLB as a world-space axis-aligned box.
  Combines all material primitives in each mesh into one box and sums triangles.

## Environment Variables

The app itself needs **no** environment variables — every tuning value lives in
`src/config/shipConfig.js` and every asset path is a literal in the source.

| Variable | Used by | Purpose | Default |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `URL` | `tests/gameplay.mjs`, `tests/smoke.mjs`, `tests/visual.mjs` | Base URL of the running dev server for browser tests. | `http://localhost:5173/` |
| `PATH` | `npm run assets` | Must contain `blender` so the asset pipeline can run headless. | system |
| `SUNNY_CDP` | `tools/exterior_audit.mjs` | Optional existing test-browser WebSocket; omitted launches its own browser. Never player-placement evidence. | unset |

`VITE_*` variables are not used; do not introduce them without updating this table.

- `URL`: optional local browser-test target; gameplay, visual, interaction and interiorJourneys default to http://localhost:5173/.
- `ROOM_FILTER`: optional interiorJourneys diagnostic room/stair name filter; omitted in npm test.
- `NAV_DEBUG`: optional roomRoutes detailed collision-boundary logging; omitted by default.

## Review repair pass (October 1)

`src/components/DoorSystem.jsx`: mounted by SceneRig; updates before physics (priority -1), publishes leaves in walking and collision-enabled flight, animates the same hinge-centred positions as the pure solver, and cleans up dynamic colliders on unmount. Ship registers tagged moving nodes in an effect with unregister cleanup. Closed/moving hatch lids are dynamic support surfaces, so the floor query follows their actual footprint. Door specs carry baseOffset for exact mesh/collider agreement.

`src/systems/doors.js`: normalizeDoor, createDoorSystem and its update/reset/publish/interaction functions manage doors/hatches. Helpers num, toThree, vec3, writeBox, travel, stepClear and clampToPlayer provide coordinate conversion and swept player clearance.

`docs/review-findings.md`: initial implementation review and repair verification, with screenshots in docs/review/. `tools/sunny_interiors.py`: Frame (x/y/z/p/station), metre-scale furniture helpers and build_interiors author furnished rooms, walls, doors and access on the enlarged hull. Low-level helpers L, _merge, rbox, rpole, panel and peg build local-frame geometry; wall_run/door_frame/door/hatch author openings and runtime metadata; stair_flight/switchback provide metre-scale stairs; aquarium supplies a contained tank.

Geometry repair: `open_volume_slot` clips doorway/shaft volumes from imported geometry, retaining material assignments and other faces. `retread_flights` and `rebuild_stair_rails` accept suffixed retread names. `switchback` now reverses direction with a full cross-landing; `stair_flight` inclines rails and excludes decorative risers from wall collision. `add_fittings`' local `shaft` cuts all overlapping deck layers for stair headroom. Hatches use perimeter frames and carry exact thickness/baseOffset; door handles parent to moving leaves. `peg` accepts height so chair/table/tank legs reach their supported tops. `_merge` retains book spine materials. build_interiors aligns transverse walls and doors, central B1 Dock, aft Energy Room, surrounding aquarium side/rear tanks and overhead service tank, women's washroom, gun closets and attic bath access. `build_assets.py` exports full-precision door parameters.

Regression repair: assets.test.mjs checks current interiors schema, all reference rooms, moving door nodes, hatch count, human furniture heights and contained aquarium water. gameplay.mjs uses interiors.levels.B1 and current bulkhead names. stairFlights includes Blender retread suffixes in original flight groups, so added steps are exercised.

Floor support iterates the cached combined static/dynamic list without per-frame array allocation. Medical bed legs reach the frame.

`tests/doors.test.mjs`: pure door/hatch regressions, executed by npm test. Tests every actual leaf against its metadata collider, approach opening, safe doorway occupancy, closing, closed-hatch support, open-hatch clearance and moving swing collision; no named helpers.

Follow-up geometry checks: exterior directly authored transforms are flushed before bounds operations; the smile is ray-fitted to the actual muzzle. Both garden flights get final-metre retreads. ForedeckSupport supports the helm deck. Kitchen upper stair shaft sits between mikan trees. Pipeline retains moving-leaf hardware parentage and includes the stern hut roof in solid collision. Hatch interaction reaches down one body height for approaches from below. Floor slab assertion accepts the existing 0.18 m interior slabs alongside 0.60 m outdoor cells.

`tests/roomRoutes.mjs`: roomRoutes builds a 20 cm layered navigation grid and flood-fills from actual lawn spawn through static geometry and parked open doors/hatches. Local key and blocked helpers index solids and account for tread support; all room volumes must contain reachable standing cells. Its direct entry writes /tmp/sunny-room-routes.json as route diagnostics. These planned paths supplement, rather than replace, controller-driven verification.

Final route repairs: human-scale hatch lids slide across the stairwell, keeping the upper-flight headroom clear. Fore and aft door approach decks and lawn side walkways bridge source enlargement gaps. Fore F2 walls stop below the foredeck; exterior toilets move into the stairwell rather than overlapping bunks. Gun closets and washrooms stay inside hull-fitted floors. The asset pipeline writes to public/.models-staging and publishes the three completed outputs after success, preserving current assets on Python/export failures.

`tests/interiorJourneys.mjs`: controller-driven room journeys from lawn spawn along roomRoutes paths. Uses actual WASD movement via driveTo and physical E key hatch interaction; trips return down the same stairs to the lawn. Captures failed positions/screenshots and runtime errors. URL optionally sets the local preview and ROOM_FILTER optionally limits diagnostic runs to one room name; both are consumed by this test only.

### Manga floor-plan correction — October 1, 2026
- `tools/sunny_interiors.py` / `build_interiors`: central six-sector Soldier Dock aligned with exterior channels; fore storage; port Franky/starboard Usopp aft workshops; aft Energy Room. F2 dining is port, kitchen starboard, infirmary/pantry behind them; clear doors and serving-bar opening. `bunk`: ceiling-attached ropes and real ladder rungs. `bathtub`: large hollow tiled bath with contained water. `_merge`/`bookcase`: palette retained on all six book faces. `hatch`: lid owns its moving hinges/ring. Library shelves/bench fit the hut, window frames surround the panes. Inner timber fore lining covers exterior decorative undersides.
- `tools/sunny_exterior.py` / `add_fittings`: relocated toilets/workbench clear entrances; fore and aft entrance landings; guard cutouts scoped to stair exits; B1 stair beside dock; kitchen access in kitchen; helm console leaves a walkable approach.
- `src/components/DoorSystem.jsx` / `DoorSystem`: compares copied hatch HUD state, avoiding reused info-object aliasing.
- `src/systems/materials.js` / `authoredMaterial`: glass and contained tank water use transparent, depth-write-free materials so fish and room geometry remain visible; retained existing surface treatment elsewhere.

- `tests/roomRoutes.mjs` / `roomRoutes`: exact footprint support from nearby floor boxes; ceilings considered during connectivity search; floating-point-tolerant straight route coalescing. Offline planned routes must be followed by real controller verification in `interiorJourneys.mjs`.
- `src/systems/collision.js`: documentation corrected to describe dynamic hatch support.

`roomRoutes` records actual footprint support heights and limits descent transitions to 0.85 m (the controller permits STEP_HEIGHT + 0.10 m supported descents). One 0.4 m grid edge can bridge sub-cell landing gaps that the 0.34 m footprint physically spans.

### Reference details and access — October 1, 2026
`tools/sunny_interiors.py` adds `curved_aquarium` (elliptical segmented panes, contained water prisms, curved frame, fish/rock/coral props; nested point/prism helpers construct shared bounds). Main mast/cupboard/lift aligns on both floors, fore lockers sit behind hanging bunks, washrooms are beside entrances, and kotatsu has a real sunken floor. `bathtub` uses an open rim and tiled shell. `tools/sunny_exterior.py` / `add_fittings` adds the bedroom StorageHatch and full switchback down to storage; toilets clear stair treads and women's entrance. `tests/assets.test.mjs` verifies four hatches, curved/rectangular tank containment, central dock and side-by-side dining/kitchen.

`tools/sunny_interiors.py` / `circular_lining`: segmented cream walls, timber dado and true glazed apertures inside the library/bath, hiding external roof paint from interior views. Library entrance panels remain clear; the attic has a timber inner ceiling below the existing roof. Main mast uses the shared pole API with an explicit obstacle tag.

Final physical transition correction: `PlayerController` allows supported descents up to STEP_HEIGHT + 0.10 m, so walking off the footprint of several overlapping stair treads does not trigger the old 0.55 m void guard. `roomRoutes` uses the controller's rectangular footprint and contact epsilon and permits 0.85 m supported descents; output heights use the actual footprint support. Imported door/stair cuts extend 0.15 m below the threshold to remove a 2 cm source-collider lip that trapped real walks. Controller collision against walls and sea rescue remain active.

Final reference/access pass: `build_interiors` exports post-timeskip dockChannels 1–6 and their contents, with geometric bay labels. It adds the second men's entrance, full-width cannon partitions, a separate Bath Washroom volume and a cross-passage beside the bath stair shaft so Bath means the actual tub room in tests. `add_fittings` bridges the second fore doorway. `useStore` keeps hasPlayerPose; `PlayerController` restores saved position/yaw/pitch on Explore remount; `gameplay.mjs` verifies this after walking away from spawn. `package.json` assets uses Blender --python-exit-code 1 so a Python exception makes npm report failure.

### Complete journey verification
`tests/roomRoutes.mjs` / `roomRoutes` now contains nested `between` (connected BFS between supported nodes), `compress` (straight-waypoint reduction), `fromSpawn` (root-path extraction), and `at` (node matching near exported stair treads). It returns individual lawn paths, a continuous room-tour path and return path, plus forced first/middle/last/exit journeys through all nine authored interior flights. NAV_DEBUG optionally prints detailed boundary diagnostics. `tests/interiorJourneys.mjs` / `walk` drives WASD and E through those routes, captures stalls and verifies grounded destinations and return descents. ROOM_FILTER limits diagnostic runs; final npm test runs every route. `tests/stairFlights.mjs` / `driveTo` allows 100 ms settling after releasing movement. `tests/run-all.mjs` is the single nine-suite entry point including both navigation and actual interior journey suites.

`docs/interior-reference.md`: reference contract for room placement, post-timeskip details and explicit modelling/access approximations (documentation; no functions). `docs/exterior-reference.md`: current 2.0× dimensions, dynamic doors, furnished-room reference link and full validation scope.

`tools/collision_glb.py` / `write_box_glb`: deterministic pure-stdlib writer for named axis-aligned Y-up collision boxes; shared index accessor, per-box positions, strict positive bounds, standard padded GLB chunks. `tools/build_assets.py` converts Blender min/max bounds and calls it for collision only, keeping visual export unchanged. Full bounds/count equivalence is checked against the preceding collision asset before rebuilding. This avoids the generic Blender exporter spending 13 minutes on 24,000 simple boxes.
Bath access correction: `build_interiors` moves the obstructing bookshelf/bench clear of the port stair, shortens its flight to leave a broad cross-passage at the top, and keeps actual Bath separate from Bath Washroom. `add_fittings` moves the kitchen stair inward so its half-landing clears the rear partition.

`door` flushes authored transforms before parenting handles to leaves, preserving latch-side placement; two hinges sit at the correct heights on the actual hinge jamb and are nonblocking hardware.
