### Active second repair pass
`tools/sunny_interiors.py`: `perimeter_lining` builds continuous chord-aligned enclosures on floor outlines; `circular_lining` uses exact elliptical chords with sash windows and a shared doorway cut; `hut_roof` builds a closed striped domed attic roof. `build_interiors` replaces damaged imported hut/arch/window geometry, encloses B1/fore/aft/aquarium rooms, adds supported ceilings, clears bath shafts and relocates sink/toilet. `sink`, `sofa`, `bunk`, `kotatsu`, and library `bench` construction now provide physical support/attachments.
`tools/sunny_exterior.py`: `repair_symmetry` reflects the malformed lateral mane petal about the measured face centre. `add_exterior` puts all three mikan trees beside the library approach. `add_fittings` removes open-deck GardenWindow duplicates, mounts aquarium/dining portholes on actual facades, cuts all layers, and fills aperture corners. The red palette is warm crimson; no new texture pass. Verification pending staged rebuild and real walking.

Enclosure verification caught an AABB proxy that refilled stair/ceiling holes and extended 2.5 cm above the bath floor. Ceilings now use thin sampled floor slabs with preserved shafts, and `build_assets.py` chooses 18 cm depth for them. `assets.test.mjs` checks every replacement library wall/window segment against its full-height solid collider and rejects unsupported garden rings/imported hut arches; mast surface assertions remain. `hut_roof` retains authored multi-material tags.

### Base geometry additions, 2026-10-01

`tools/sunny_details.py` adds `dock_vehicle` (six reference-led, human-metre vehicle silhouettes; nested cube/ball/tube/wheel helpers), `tea_set` (supported teapot/lid/handle/spout/cups) and `wall_art` (geometry poster cues, detailed character likeness deferred). `sunny_interiors.build_interiors` tags carousel contents by channel, records physical dock bounds independently of static collision, encloses both bedroom washrooms, fixes vanity frame/support and shelf bottle support, and places posters against the gun-closet partition. `sunny_exterior.PALETTE` adds blue vehicle paint and the selector is normal to its console face. `build_assets.py` excludes moving dock geometry from the static proxy and exports dockCentre/dockSolids; finishedRooms stays false until full physical/visual review is complete. Dynamic dock collision must be mounted before validating these rebuilt assets.

### Navigation runtime and browser readiness, 2026-10-01

`src/systems/navigation.js`: CHANNELS/LOG_BEARINGS identify the six models and three illustrative world destinations; voyage holds heading/throttle/coordinates/cola/dock state. resetVoyage resets all instruments, shipYaw maps compass bearing to render yaw, rotatePoint transforms ship-local positions, dockBounds moves physical bounds with models, advanceVoyage integrates steering/speed/distance and pauses an occupied carousel, startBurst consumes cola. Bearings are simulation targets, not sourced canonical island coordinates. Sailing uses a floating ocean origin: the ship/camera rotate in world space, walking/doors retain ship-local physics, and Ocean advances wave phase with travelled position and inverse-rotates hull masking.

`NavigationSystem.jsx` mounts after PlayerController, subscribes E for nearby helm/Log Pose/selector/lever, G for Burst, R reset; its frame drives carousel meshes/dynamic collision, instruments, local-to-world Explore camera and throttled HUD state. `Ship.jsx` uses navigation yaw; `PlayerController.jsx` suppresses walking wish at helm and restores R reset. `useStore.js` adds navigation/setNavigation; `UI.jsx` displays heading/speed/channel/cola and contextual controls. `collision.setDynamic(entries,source)` combines independently owned door/dock layers; clearDynamic removes all. `App.jsx` mounts navigation.

`tests/roomRoutes.mjs` includes dock's initial physical bounds. Browser journeys/gameplay/visual wait for DOM plus the loaded scene rather than network-idle (Vite's live connection prevented readiness); visual closes its browser in finally and treats runtime errors as failure. These changes preserve all movement, grounding and collision assertions.

Navigation drives the two existing `Dock1_shutter` discs and attached channel number geometry through a 6.2 m vertical slide. Imported hull/rim apertures still require visible verification before claiming an operational vehicle launch passage. Reset (R) resets voyage and the player's lawn spawn.

`build_assets.py` uses per-face collision sections for new shell linings and clipped facade/serving partitions, preserving each doorway/window aperture instead of recreating it with a full-object bounding box. `tests/navigation.test.mjs` validates heading/distance, inverse local/world transforms, cola use, occupied carousel pause and rotated bounds; run-all includes this suite. Gameplay now explicitly waits for the loaded scene before its initial title click.

`assets.test.mjs` physicalWall validates full-height bounds for both intact box partitions and face-section walls with apertures. Additional structural checks require all six vehicle silhouettes/physical bounds, four tea cups/teapot and sampled (not full-box) ceilings. Original height/body/radius/collision and route assertions are retained.

`src/systems/sails.js` matches base crimson #b91d25 and multiplies ambient/backlight into cloth colour, preventing blue sky additive light from washing red/black stripes into pink/gray. Sail wind geometry remains unchanged. Structural vehicle checks require channel-specific silhouette features (horse/sheep/shark/rhino/dinosaur/pool) plus physical bounds; the ceiling test distinguishes actual room slabs from bunk attachment beams.

Actual Bath journey on generation 2 stopped at x=8.22, z=-14 on the final exterior aft tread. The new Aquarium shell corner began before the next tread's support footprint and blocked the walker despite a grid path. `sunny_exterior.add_fittings` now cuts the body-clearance envelope through both top stair approaches, starting 0.9 m below F2A. This geometry change requires the next asset generation; passing BathStair grid anchors alone was not accepted as walking proof.

The Library lining's entrance cut was running before Blender evaluated freshly authored object transforms, collapsing those segments to the origin. circular_lining now flushes transforms once before its cut batch. A new structural placement assertion verifies every lining segment lies at its library level/radial location as well as having collision. `add_fittings` flushes before its new aft-shell cut batch and encloses both fore toilets with sides/back/ceiling; they are included as intended room volumes for walking verification. `sunny_details.wall_art` differentiates straw hat/green hair/long nose/orange hair as base geometry cues, with detailed likeness explicitly incomplete. Library adds a mounted clock and supported mapping chart/island/meridian geometry.

Log Pose needles are authored parallel to world north (Blender +Y) on three small height-separated planes; NavigationSystem applies -world bearing minus ship yaw so each retains its world destination while the ship turns. The three colours identify needles in the selected-needle readout. The dock obstacle pass now explicitly skips sunny_dynamic meshes (the earlier skip affected only the floor pass).

`roomRoutes` now builds `approaches` using every exported fore lower tread, followed by the women's doorway and a cabin destination, for both viewing sides. `interiorJourneys` drives these extra lawn/stair/cabin/reverse-lawn journeys, treats them as separate forced approaches, and captures every successful walking destination as `docs/review/walk-*.png`. Diagnostic flight captures remain separately named.

Forced interior flights now anchor every tread, plus supported entry and exit, so a planned alternative path cannot establish that only the first/middle/last tread is reachable. Actual WASD traversal and reverse descents are still required.

`build_assets.add_box` now returns plain (name,low,high) physical bounds, converted directly by write_box_glb. The invisible collision proxy no longer creates tens of thousands of Blender scene objects or re-links them between collections; visual export evaluates only the actual ship. This preserves the exact box dimensions/names and deterministic writer contract while removing unnecessary scene-evaluation work. add_mesh remains the generic mesh helper.

`stairFlights.walkJourney` adds optional returnToLawn=false for a control operation beginning with the entire continuous lawn-to-helm walk. Existing outdoor journeys retain return descents by default. `tests/navigation.mjs` then operates E/WASD/G at the physical helm, checks local player stability/world ship yaw/ocean masking/sailing distance, walks around the console to operate selector/lever/Log Pose and verifies R resets voyage/player. Captures are walking evidence; no teleport is used. The complete runner includes this browser suite.

The first generation-4 attempt failed before publishing because the new Ship Log text omitted its required rotation argument. The call now specifies a flat page rotation; published generation-3 assets remained intact.

The initial navigation browser run walked to the helm and passed steering/sailing/Burst before its selector assertion failed: the test added the wheel's Z offset twice, stopping outside E's 1.65 m reach. The console waypoints now use its actual relative offset and pass around its solid footprint. No interaction range or assertion was relaxed.

The corrected library entrance legitimately removes entire lower wall/dado segments inside the opening. `build_assets.py` now removes and records empty meshes after authoring/cuts, before bounds and collision classification. This prevents a None-bounds failure while retaining all nonempty wall faces; the source FBX is unchanged.

The lever is approached from the open inboard face via the east side of its solid console. An outboard approach intersects the ship's fore rail; the test now walks around the console to the supported inboard operation point, preserving rail collision.

Sukhjot's follow-up identified both lateral mane petals as wrong. Comparing actual incoming/current GLB bounds showed Rectangle006 was the malformed depth-oriented petal; Rectangle004 was originally intact. The earlier copy direction duplicated Rectangle006's defect onto Rectangle004. repair_symmetry now preserves original Rectangle004 and mirrors it onto Rectangle006 around the measured face centre. The original FBX is the input on every rebuild; other mane petals, face and bones remain preserved. A front-view screenshot and radial/symmetry assertion are required after rebuilding.

`assets.test.mjs` now verifies both lateral mane petals have greater radial than depth extent, share height and mirror around the face. PlayerController exposes an automatic-recovery counter; interiorJourneys rejects any walking segment that invokes the fall/sea rescue, so a hidden reposition cannot count as continuous walking proof. Manual respawn remains limited to the beginning of a separate route.

Unsupported freestanding tool boards and Usopp's observation-deck drawing board now have timber stands/feet meeting their floors (`tool_board` and `add_fittings`). Their existing human-sized board dimensions are preserved.

### Exterior-first scope, 2026-10-01
Sukhjot paused further room repair. exteriorConfig.json now uses 2.2 experience scale (85.8 m length, 123.2 m height); player height, eye height, radius, speeds and human fittings retain their metre dimensions. materials.authoredMaterial now renders stable authored base colours without the earlier procedural grain/relief shader; surfaceNoiseTexture and legacy fallback material functions remain available for a later texture pass. Gold roughness is 0.46. App uses soft PCF shadows; Lighting covers the enlarged silhouette with an 80 m half-width shadow frustum, 2048 map and adjusted bias to reduce self-shadow acne. These changes require motion/stop browser comparison and asset rebuild; no room completion is claimed.

sunny_exterior.subtract_plan_polygon clips a horizontal face outside a convex XY footprint into disjoint pieces; its nested clip preserves cut-edge Z. remove_exterior_overlaps assigns priority to actual treads, landings, lawn and exterior bridging surfaces, buckets their upward triangles by height, and trims coincident horizontal source/overlay faces within 4 mm. Vertical structure and unrelated room geometry are retained. restore_exterior runs this after all final transformations/fittings; add_fittings anchors ForedeckSupport footprint to experienceScale so enlargement retains physical helm support. overlap removal records sunny_removed_overlap_faces per changed mesh. Rebuild and exported overlap audit are required.

Sails now project reference markings in ship coordinates through uShipInverse, keeping stripes/hat/flag attached during bobbing and steering. Ship frame updates the parent transform and shared inverse matrix before sail uniforms; wind is transformed into ship/mesh coordinates. Fine sail weave/seam noise is deferred; stripe edges use screen derivatives to avoid hard subpixel jumps. remove_exterior_overlaps uses full polygon fan area when rejecting degenerate output (collinear initial cut vertices are valid). Syntax scan/build and real sailing/motion checks follow.

Ship updates only the bob parent world matrix for sail projection, avoiding an extra full traversal of thousands of meshes each frame. Removed the unused sail weave hash after pausing weave. Fresh scan confirms no authored surface onBeforeCompile shader remains and sail markings use the shared inverse transform.

add_fittings.exterior_floor anchors exterior approach/sidewalk/aft landing/helm support footprints to the enlargement while keeping thickness, head clearance and fittings human-sized. Exterior stair clearance cuts move with those footprints. Aquarium doorway bridge stays on its existing fixed facade. No new room design work was performed. tests/visual.mjs scopes press/release helpers with their page inside try, fixing a ReferenceError introduced by the earlier cleanup.

tests/exterior.test.mjs independently inspects transformed GLB triangles: visit reads horizontal triangles; intersectionArea clips triangle intersections in plan. It rejects cross-mesh overlap above 1 cm² within 3 mm of exterior lawn/landing/approach/helm planes. It tests actual exported geometry rather than only pipeline counters. run-all includes it without removing existing suites.

tests/exteriorMotion.mjs uses actual WASD movement on the lawn, asserts grounded stop/displacement/no automatic recovery and <5 cm stationary drift, captures during motion and after a 5-second stop, compares stationary lower-deck pixels, and records three Inspect angles. It flies to the lion using movement keys without teleport; flight screenshots are appearance diagnostics only. Its sleep helper bounds individual waits to five seconds. Browser closes in finally and runtime errors fail. run-all adds it while preserving every room test.

build_assets.py skips the expensive source-only spawn scoring when the authored exterior supplies its explicit lawn spawn. Previously it scored thousands of candidates with 16 scene rays each, then discarded that result and wrote the fixed lawn spawn anyway. The exported arrival formula is identical. Source-only fallback retains the complete headroom/open-space search. This changes rebuild cost, not spawn/collision or test thresholds.

Ocean fragment shader fades ripple normals by their screen-space derivative footprint and broadens/reduces the tight sun specular. This removes distant salt-like undersampling during orbit/walking while preserving Gerstner displacement, contained hull cutout, finite ocean and horizon geometry. No texture assets are added. The superseded generation-7 staging build was terminated before publication; final generation 8 includes scaled approaches.

Reference comparison of the actual Inspect capture identified swapped lookout colour placement: tall dome faces were cream while the underside was red/yellow. paint_source now classifies Cylinder006 upward roof faces by normal for red/gold stripes, keeping vertical window-band faces cream; Cylinder005 underside is cream. This preserves geometry and uses the anime roof/band/underside placement, replacing unreliable polygon-centre height classification.

tests/navigation.mjs markingSpace captures each sail/flag model-to-ship matrix before/after actual helm steering. It asserts all reference markings retain their cloth coordinates after ship yaw, in addition to existing physical foot, water-cutout and navigation checks. This detects pattern swimming rather than only the presence of a shader uniform.

The exported overlap test caught the last starboard fore tread overlapping its landing because that imported top has reversed face winding. remove_exterior_overlaps now identifies stair tops by their measured height regardless of winding, keeping positive normals for ordinary floor masks. Safety hull beam inset is a fixed 0.6 m on each side, so the unchanged 1.5 m coverage assertion still holds after enlargement; hull top remains below all decks. assets.test library placement now checks the actual library floor bounds in both horizontal axes and its height, replacing a stale 2.0-scale x=20..33 envelope; it still rejects origin-collapsed walls and adds transverse floor containment.

Enlargement verification found the upper fore flight blocked by the fixed cabin frontage/ceiling at x=-18.35. add_fittings now cuts body/head clearance following every actual upper fore tread through only that frontage/ceiling; build_assets preserves these cut frontage faces as sectioned collision rather than refilling the passage with an AABB. This repairs exterior stair access without designing rooms. walkJourney now routes the starboard garden journey around the solid lawn dock hatch/rail at a waypoint derived from its actual leaf bounds; all actual stair ascents, reverse descents, collision and return-to-lawn assertions remain. The new spawn aligned with the fixed hatch rail; walking through it was an invalid route.

### Exterior implementation session — October 1, 2026
`tools/exterior_audit.mjs`: `category(name)` maps evidence families; `inventory()` records every exported mesh's bounds, normalized station, nautical side, count, tags and measured floor contact in JSON/CSV. The script captures matched top/port/starboard/front/stern/quarters/lookout/garden views from the actual preview. Top hides canvas/flags/ratlines; all captures are Inspect evidence only. Runtime errors and renderer context are recorded. `SUNNY_CDP` optionally connects to an existing dedicated test browser.
`tools/exterior_plates.py`: `panel()` fits supplied source/screenshot panels without modifying the inputs; `plate()` produces labelled top/side/front comparisons, orientation, user scale exceptions and evidence limits.
`docs/exterior-feature-ledger.md`: all 42 exterior entries map current meshes to intended station/orientation/count/support, supplied reference keys, conflict decisions and unresolved status; links the per-mesh measured CSV/JSON and pre-edit annotated plates. Documentation only.
`sunny_interiors.build_interiors`: the exposed `InteriorMainMast` extension now measures the actual enlarged `Cylinder007` centre/radius/base and flushes its transform before its existing cupboard cut. It joins the structural mast instead of being baked at the origin. No room furnishing/layout changes.
`tools/sunny_accuracy.py`: final-metre exterior pass. `discard()` removes superseded detail nodes; `replace()` replaces source data with evaluated world geometry and optional UVs; `cut_prism()` subtracts convex apertures with retained materials; `circular_cut()` supplies circular wall-prism planes; `curved_sails()` rebuilds two deployed white sails and two striped gaff panels in the existing third node; `hull_ports()` replaces seven blind caps and authors both sides' attached pale/dark apertures; `burst_barrel()` rebuilds the source stern barrel with stave construction, hoops and recessed bore; `leaf_mesh()` constructs attached folded blades; `citrus_fruit()` makes oblate fruit/depressed pole/calyx/stem/branch contact; `reference_tree()` builds supported branched vegetation at human scale; `plants()` replaces two lawn trees, the three-pot mikan cluster and flower/Pop Green beds; `apply_exterior_accuracy()` orchestrates the pass and returns evidence/mechanism metadata.
`sunny_exterior.restore_exterior` calls the accuracy pass after existing interiors/fittings/rails, before overlap trim; room authoring is unchanged apart from the exposed mast correction. `build_assets.py` includes its `exterior.mechanisms` metadata in the same staged generation as visual and collision assets.
`docs/review/{before,after}-exterior-inventory.{json,csv}`: generated all-mesh measurements, including unresolved objects; `{before,after}-{top,port,starboard,front,stern,bow-quarter,stern-quarter,lookout,garden}.png`, comparison plates and appearance-context JSON are generated diagnostic evidence, never walking proof.
`sunny_accuracy.merge_details()` batches attached branch/twig/leaf meshes by tree and material to limit draw calls. `curved_sails()` now reconstructs the three longitudinal main-mast gaffs from common mast attachment and stern endpoint, replacing the source's transverse middle spar; both striped panels follow these attachment edges with their own UVs.
`sunny_accuracy.helm_controls()` replaces the separate console with H's integrated numbered drum/knob and lower pillar lever and records real control targets; removes unsupported extra observation-deck benches. `rolling_dock()` cuts both hull apertures, builds 32 timber slats per opening, clips digits 0–6 into their slat bands, tags rolling parts, and authors paired channel-0 paddle geometry. `lookout()` rebuilds the shallow striped dome, glazed band, four dormers, attached flag/pole and rear loudspeaker. `lion_mechanisms()` retains/tags all seven mane petals, separates a lower muzzle jaw, cuts the Gaon aperture, and supplies hinge/outlet metadata. `deck_details()` places the swing on the physical port tree, builds a supported starboard U-section slide and moves the flue to the plan's starboard kitchen side. `stern_lanterns()` replaces the two measured source lantern stations/stray brackets with cages, glazed panes and supported brackets. These reconstructions require exported comparison and motion/collision verification; hidden counts and exact actuator dimensions remain unresolved.
`sunny_accuracy.library_roof()` reshapes only the existing exterior roof, adds four arched glazed dormers with real cut roof apertures and a flat top vent/rim; it does not furnish/rearrange the rooms. Gaff rebuilding preserves the measured original +X endpoint so the overall configured 85.8 m envelope is retained.
`navigation.js`: `initial()` defines complete reset state; `channelName()` labels 0 paddles and 1–6 existing vehicles; `channelAngle()` maps carousel targets; `shutterPose()` computes slat/digit translation and roller rotation with separated winding layers. `advanceVoyage()` gates carousel on closed shutters/clear turntable, pauses occupied closure, animates jaw/mane/paddles, spends simulated paddle cola, and advances a coherent Burst arc. `startBurst()` spends three barrels for a finite 6 s, 32 m high/240 m range **simulation**; `fireGaon()` spends three for uncompensated recoil/air blast, without damage simulation. Canonical flight distances and compensated Gaon cost are not claimed for this preview. Existing `resetVoyage`, `shipYaw`, `rotatePoint`, `dockBounds` remain public.
`public/artwork/straw-hat-jolly-roger.svg`: essential sail/flag paint manually drawn from supplied O/H/C, with rounded bone ends, skull/nose/teeth and hat crown/brim/band. No functions. Base artwork precedes the deferred surface texture pass.
`sails.emblemTexture()` loads shared sRGB vector artwork with mipmaps, linear filtering and anisotropy. `makeSailMaterial()` accepts authored UV/flag state; its vertex shader pins all sail edges and flag pole edge, its fragment shader uses the complete drawn Roger on deployed foresail/flags and reduces cloth specular. `Ship()` uses exported authored UV tags and applies simulated lift/pitch in Explore, preserving the shared inverse transform. `Ocean()` clears the hull cutout during airborne Burst through `uShipLift`, restoring it on landing; far and near materials share the uniform. Surface grain/weave remains deferred while geometry acceptance is open.
`NavigationSystem.jsx`: `pivotNode()` rotates an authored/baked mesh about its real pivot; `movingBounds()` transforms the same corners for dynamic shutter collision. `NavigationSystem()` discovers exported dynamic tags; rolls matching numbered slat portions, gates channel-0 paddles, rotates seven petals/hinged jaw, follows airborne ship pose with camera and restrained air-ring effects, and derives interaction from actual exported control targets with near/aim selection. E operates the integrated station; G/T/J/K operate Burst/Chicken/mouth/Gaon while holding the helm. Log Pose text explicitly labels illustrative bearings. Closure occupancy and turntable occupancy independently guard physical movement. `UI()` displays fractional cola and the complete controls/simulation label. `rolling_dock()` exports shared slat/digit pivots and tags hidden channel-0 axles with their wheels.
`curved_sails()` measures each white sail's supporting yard centre/span and wraps ties around the actual spar section; this avoids detached small rings and bounds extending past only one yard end. Fresh exported scale/centering checks follow each rebuild.
`tests/navigation.test.mjs` now verifies the Burst apex/landing and three-barrel costs, roller winding/occupied closure, closed-shutter carousel interlock, all seven selector states, Chicken reverse, Gaon jaw/recoil and powered paddles. `stairFlights.walkJourney()` records recovery counts at every walking waypoint/flight/return and requires grounded, recovery-free motion from the initial spawn; existing ascent/descent tolerances and real WASD input remain unchanged.
`assets.test.mjs` retains all room/source checks and adds an FBX SHA-256 invariant, exported mast floor/join checks, fourteen corrected ports, dense authored canvas UVs/two gaff panels, exact clustered human-sized mikan pots/oblate fruit attachments, 64 dynamic rolling slats/seven digits, integrated controls, seven mane pivots/jaw and twelve lantern panes. `curved_sails()` centres the two envelope-defining source yards by 0.099 m without shifting deck/room metadata; original centering tolerances are retained.
Browser console review caught a React progress update caused by starting TextureLoader inside material construction. `Ship()` now Suspense-loads the shared artwork through `useTexture` before constructing materials; `sails.emblemTexture(texture)` only configures the provided cached texture and never starts a loader during render. Fresh page/console verification is required after the repair.
Matched image inspection caught inverted native GLTF V coordinates and dormers hidden inside the roof surface. `sails` now converts native V to the authored upright marking convention; both roof functions place dormer faces outside the roof and cut their apertures. `reference_tree()` distributes connected twigs/denser varied blades across branch interiors, retaining 16 fruit per mikan tree, to avoid bare spokes and hollow foliage crowns. The images, rather than console-only results, triggered these corrections.
`lion_mechanisms()` recesses the cannon behind the closed muzzle; the first diagnostic showed its rim protruding through the closed jaw. The jaw/petal silhouettes and supplied radial source petals are preserved.
`tests/navigation.mjs` now walks to the integrated exported targets, aims at each actual fitting, and operates E/WASD/T/J/G; checks airborne hull/camera/water state, stable local feet/cloth marking coordinates, rolling slats/digits and dynamic bounds, labelled simulated Log Pose, then continues over both actual fore flights down to spawn without rescue. A separate starboard spawn-to-helm walk verifies K Gaon/recoil after R refills/resets. Local `aim()` faces the fitting; `useControl()` walks to its station before interacting. Screenshots include instruments, airborne Burst, closed/open mane/mouth, return lawn and Gaon.
`sunny_accuracy.human_rails()` reconstructs garden stair handrails at 0.52/1.02 m above actual treads, resizes balcony posts, adds matching two-bar rails and opens the sourced fore entrance. All are sectioned physical rails; source-deck positions remain. `anchor_paint()` applies H's three dark stripes to the two existing gold balls, leaving unresolved rope/hawse/capstan routing explicit. `helm_controls()` places the knob at the wheel's starboard/right upper quadrant when viewed from aft toward the bow.
Exterior threshold survey found LibraryDoor at fixed x=21.95 while the actual fore wall/aperture moved to x=lcx−4.95 under enlargement. `build_interiors()` now places only that exposed door/frame at its real exterior wall; its interior furnishings/layout remain paused. `plants()` keeps all three Nami pots clustered on the port side between main mast/library, with a clear passage along the garden's aft edge (cluster stations are reconstructions, not canonical metre measurements).
The expanded navigation browser run passed all physical operations/return descents but failed its console-error assertion because initial artwork loading still began during Ship render. `Ship` now preloads both GLB and vector artwork outside rendering, retaining Suspense/cache use. A fresh full run must confirm removal of the React warning; it is not suppressed by tests.
`tests/exteriorJourneys.mjs`: `step()` drives actual walking to each waypoint and requires supported feet/no recovery, saving a stall screenshot before failure. The suite continues from a port garden ascent around every stern balcony sector, across/return through the exposed LibraryDoor, back down to F2 gallery, across it and down the starboard slide, then along both entire lawn-side passages and back. It saves complete route JSON and normal-eye captures. `run-all.mjs` adds this fourteenth suite while retaining all thirteen prior suites/room assertions. `deck_details()` anchors the slide's upper entry at x=10 on the existing gallery support rather than its formerly suspended x=7.8 end.
`exteriorMotion.mjs` retains its original strict deck-region/grounded/no-recovery assertion and adds a continuous garden ascent, real strafing views of plants/glass, matched normal-eye stopped frames five seconds apart, broad 800×520 difference/mean-change measurements, and a 3 s frame-rate sample with viewport/renderer context. These additional regions include transparent water/moving cloth backgrounds and are reported for actual image inspection, not falsely asserted wholly static. `docs/review/exterior-motion-regions.json` stores the measurements/context; new plants/glass moving/stopped PNGs accompany the existing deck/Inspect captures.
`curved_sails()` derives outer gaff endpoints/root heights from the actual two longitudinal source spars and existing middle height; panel outlines interpolate their different spans rather than inventing identical rectangular booms. The missing middle longitudinal endpoint is a documented reconstruction between those measured ends. `makeSailMaterial()` accepts `flagEdge`; `Ship()` pins the imported main flag at its +X pole edge and the rebuilt fore flag at its authored zero edge.
`sunny_accuracy.branch_office()` replaces the generic upright board with N/F3's single sloped drawing table/page at its supported station, retaining its stool. `rudder_mechanism()` identifies the measured `Line012` blade below/behind the barrel and exports its vertical steering pivot; `NavigationSystem()` turns that blade with the wheel and transforms its dynamic collider identically. Hidden steering linkage remains unresolved. `rolling_dock()` uses a 7.8 m aperture radius and appropriately large digits to match the original central oval/anime broadside relationship; that radius/roller actuator detail is a reconstruction, not a cited canonical measurement.

Preview restart verification: `npm run dev -- --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5173 --strictPort` serves the current staged/published generation used by browser suites. Architecture scale/material/sail descriptions above now reflect the active 2.2 base-colour implementation rather than the historical 1.7 procedural rendering.

### Updated phase authorization — October 1, 2026
Sukhjot supplied `stuff/Thousand Sunny at Golden Hour.png` as a realistic material/lighting quality reference. It is an image artifact (no functions), not the authority for structural placement; the supplied anime/manga/SBS images remain the design evidence. The latest instruction explicitly orders complete exterior geometry/placement/base colours/function, then exterior textures/lighting, then room-by-room interiors. Interior work is authorized only after the exterior phase is complete; current room failures remain visible until that phase.

`sunny_accuracy.anchor_paint()` now clips actual polygon bands across the transverse axis of the retained balls, fixing the jagged depth-based outline seen in the first after capture; no overlapping stripe decals. `fore_shrouds()` replaces the unsupported rectangular source grids with paired six-rope fans and human-spaced ratlines connecting the lawn mounting region to the real fore yard, recording every endpoint and batching rods per side; its nested `rod()` appends eight-sided rope geometry. `mast_platforms()` preserves the three measured mainmast platform stations/footprints/tops, rebuilds timber discs/dark rims and exposed radial timber support braces rather than opaque black bowls. Six shrouds/eight braces are disclosed reconstruction counts; hidden knots/linkages remain unresolved. `apply_exterior_accuracy()` calls these functions before collecting/exporting final meshes.

`sunny_exterior.add_fittings()` derives the aquarium shaft's fore cut boundary from its rise/run and required head clearance. It preserves the existing exposed gallery above low treads while leaving the upper stair/hatch headroom open. The first complete expanded exterior walk passed the entire balcony and library threshold/return, then stalled at the previous oversized shaft crossing at Three `[11.11,-35.62,7.73]`; tests/tolerances remain unchanged pending the next rebuild. No interior furnishing/layout changes.

`sunny_accuracy.ellipse_paint()` clips the retained bow polygons into a base painted field and perimeter band without overlay/z-fighting or moving the shell. `bow_paint()` corrects `Circle038`'s entire gold outer bow to A/C's red field/white outer edge, `Circle002`'s lion disk to black with a gold perimeter, and six incorrectly timber-painted bow bosses to gold. Band width is a reconstruction from the supplied views; the exact diagonal white panel divisions remain under review. `apply_exterior_accuracy()` invokes this base-colour correction before export.

Exterior glazing review — October 2, 2026: `sunny_interiors.circular_lining()` accepts `wide_windows`; its library-only option expands the existing seven sash bays across three continuous shell chords, from the timber dado to the header, with two horizontal crossbars. `build_interiors()` selects this option only for the externally visible library shell. This follows L's tall grouped panes rather than the preceding small isolated slits; no furnishings or room layout change, and hidden bay count remains a reconstruction. Bath lining retains its previous dimensions pending the room phase.

`sunny_exterior.add_fittings()` replaces the four Dining/four Aquarium facade circles with N/A's arched sash construction at their existing measured stations: supported sill/two jambs/segmented arch, tall glass/mullion/two crossbars and solid cream corner infills. Cuts pass through all existing facade layers and glass now blocks physical walking. The count/hidden facade rhythm remains explicitly under reference review; this does not change the separate round side-panel/lower hull ports or room furnishings.

`tests/assets.test.mjs` retains all previous assertions and adds actual fore-shroud mast/floor endpoint contact, retained anchor band/paint fields, timber/dark platform materials with mounted braces and eight arched physically glazed facade sashes. Local `materialNames()` resolves exported per-primitive paint for the bow/platform checks. Complete runner remains fourteen suites.

October 2 hardware export repair: `sunny_exterior.assign_world_mesh(obj, mesh)` captures direct child world matrices, bakes a replacement mesh with an identity transform, then restores its attached children after dependency evaluation. `open_plan_slot()`, `open_volume_slot()`, `cap_roof()` and `remove_exterior_overlaps()` use it; `sunny_accuracy.replace()` and `cut_prism()` use the same helper. The exported four hatch lids had world-baked geometry while their twelve hinges/rings fell back near the origin; this fixes the source pipeline rather than editing GLBs. `assets.test.mjs` inspects actual glTF parent relationships and requires every door handle and all twelve hatch fittings inside their leaf bounds plus 25 cm mounting clearance. Existing dynamic-parent/collision checks remain.
The helper evaluates dependencies only for meshes with attached children, avoiding needless scene updates for ordinary wall/paint cuts. Python syntax was verified without writing bytecode outside the workspace.

`exteriorJourneys.mjs` retains the original direct lawn return after the slide. A diagnostic detour was removed after exported collision inspection proved the stop was an oversized Soldier Dock shaft cut, rather than a shroud obstruction. Controller, collision and test tolerances are unchanged. All continuations use walking input with grounded/no-recovery checks.

October 2 hull paint/aperture corrections: `sunny_accuracy.repaint_prism()` partitions retained faces into painted convex regions while preserving the outside material and attached children; it adds no overlapping decal shell. `bow_paint()` adds three white divisions to the retained red outer shield from A/C, retaining the separate white edge/gold inner disk. `hull_bands()` restores black material to the existing curved `Box118/119` side bands and `Box121` stern return, and partitions two dark straps on the retained rudder. `apply_exterior_accuracy()` includes these before export. Division/strap widths are reconstructed from perspective evidence, not claimed surveyed dimensions.
`rolling_dock()` now cuts the full measured thickness of exterior scroll/moulding meshes, whose inner skins sat beyond its previous three-metre cut and left an old red oval around the new aperture. The main floor-bearing hull retains the scoped outer-shell cut so the lawn remains supported. No room furnishing/layout changes.
`sunny_exterior.add_fittings()` derives the Soldier Dock shaft's aft boundary from its actual half-flight rise/run and 2.1 m headroom, preserving the lawn above low treads instead of removing the whole run. `assets.test.mjs` adds actual floor support at the failed return station, existing dark moulding/rudder strap materials, and the three bow-shield white divisions. These checks supplement unchanged walking/collision tests; they do not establish full appearance fidelity.

`sunny_accuracy.anchor_ropes()` raycasts each retained stowed ball's bow-shield support, cuts a small actual hawse aperture, adds the supported gold lip/dark bore and a short curved rope ending at the ball. Missing measured support fails the pipeline. It returns exterior anchor stations/endpoints; the concealed capstan run remains explicitly unresolved. `scalloped_soffit()` rebuilds the two retained lookout/library underside shells as smooth flared lobes within their original bounds, following N's petal supports. Eight hidden lobes are a disclosed reconstruction count. `apply_exterior_accuracy()` invokes both and includes anchors in mechanism metadata; `assets.test.mjs` checks the actual retained ball contact, rope/end counts and both soffit tags. These are geometry/base-colour changes; no texture or room furnishing pass yet.

### Lookout access implementation — October 2, 2026
`src/systems/climbing.js` supplies pure, ship-local climb paths: `normalizeClimbs()` validates Blender points/converts axes/caches cumulative metre lengths; `pointAt()` interpolates a bounded position; `closestOnPath()` projects physical progress; `createClimbingSystem()` owns endpoint-only `nearest()`/`attach()`, human-speed and frame-clamped `propose()`, collision-dependent `commit()` and `detach()`. Local `dist()`/`clamp()` support the calculations. Proposals never move the player or commit progress before collision succeeds. This module is not yet wired into the live player until the current complete verification run finishes.
`tests/climbing.test.mjs` verifies invalid/non-finite/zero-length rejection, source-axis conversion, continuous path corners/endpoints, reach-limited attachment, no midpoint jump, bounded hitch travel, uncommitted blocked proposals and reverse descent. It will join the single runner with the runtime wiring; no existing suite is removed.

`tools/sunny_accuracy.py:lookout_access()` derives the lookout floor from its actual window-band sill, cuts a genuine hatch opening through the deck/retained soffit, registers `CrowNestHatch`, adds N's exposed guide rope/hanging ladder with human-sized rungs, makes the existing glazing physically solid, and records a human-centre climb polyline and F3C level. Exact offsets are clearance reconstructions, not official measurements; gym furnishings remain deferred. `apply_exterior_accuracy()` runs it after the soffit repair and exports `climbs`. `sunny_exterior.restore_exterior()` snapshots the opening registry again after exterior accuracy authoring. `build_assets.py` includes explicit `sunny_access_slot` columns when excluding artificial floor-edge guards, preserving the genuine ladder hole. Runtime/asset verification follows completion of the in-progress full suite.
`climbing.js:createClimbingSystem().restore(saved,position)` restores Inspect-mode climb progress only when the current physical pose lies within 10 cm of that saved path position; it rejects non-finite/out-of-range progress and remote poses. `climbing.test.mjs` verifies both matching restoration and remote/invalid rejection.
`src/systems/interactions.js:createInteractionBus()` owns `subscribe(fn,priority)` and `emit()`; dispatch preserves equal-priority registration order and stops only on an explicit `true` result. High-priority ladder grabbing can consume E while active climbing passes hatch interaction through. `climbing.test.mjs` covers consumption, pass-through and unsubscription to prevent double actions.

`sunny_exterior.add_fittings()` moves the two previously disconnected outdoor toilet shells from the F2 landing flanks to the lawn beneath the foremast stairwells, as described beside the supplied F1/F3 stair drawings and in the [Sunny reference](https://onepiece.fandom.com/wiki/Thousand_Sunny). Their forward-facing doorway planes share the new shell frames; room metadata now reports F1/correct port-starboard names. Existing human-sized toilet/sink fixtures move with the shells, and their ceiling remains physically solid. The 1.60×1.70 m shells/2.10 m clearance are reconstruction dimensions constrained by the actual stair underside. This is exterior shell/entry correction, not a furnishing pass; fresh collision/threshold checks remain required.

`sunny_accuracy.fit_scroll_paint()` measures vertical silhouette sections of each retained white scroll border, clips its red panel to that existing contour, and preserves the mounted panel geometry. Its nested `clip()` clips one polygon against an arbitrary plane. This removes the old displaced red oval/spur outside the sourced U border without inventing a replacement outline. `apply_exterior_accuracy()` calls it before ports/dock apertures. The construction2 screenshots showed the remnant even after full-depth aperture cuts; this additional source repair requires fresh export/view validation.

`useInput.js:onInteract(fn,priority)`/`fireInteract()` now use the tested interaction bus; existing default-priority subscribers retain their fan-out order. `App.SceneRig()` passes authored climb paths to `PlayerController`. The controller memoizes its path system, restores matching saved progress, handles endpoint grabs at priority 100, passes active-climb E to reachable hatches, and sweeps bounded human-speed physical movement with the existing collision solver before committing progress. It releases only on actual endpoint footing, exposes read-only climb state and keeps 1.7 m eyes; F leaves climbing intentionally. `useStore` adds `climbPose` to `setPlayer()` and equality-guarded `setNearClimb()` text. `UI()` shows nearby rope/ladder controls and real hatch open/close prompts; no per-frame climb-pose subscription. `exteriorJourneys.mjs` walks around the real Soldier Dock stair opening at its verified lawn support before traversing/returning through the starboard side passage; tolerances and no-recovery checks remain. `assets.test.mjs` now requires the fifth lookout hatch and all fifteen attached hinge/ring fittings, retaining each original moving-door/collider check.
The controller's explicit reset/debug placement also detach its climb state. `tests/run-all.mjs` includes the pure climb/interaction suite (fifteen suites pending the continuous browser climb addition); no previous suite is removed.
`tests/climbing.mjs` adds a full real-keyboard lawn-to-rope, hanging-ladder, closed/open hatch, lookout-floor walk and reverse descent to spawn. Local `read()` captures actual physical state; `walk()` requires grounded/no-recovery footing; `climb()` samples bounded speed, progress, stalls and recovery without placement/flight APIs. It checks Inspect pose preservation, two closed-hatch stops and actual E reopening, writes continuous trace/screenshots, and captures failure evidence before closing Chrome. It joins `run-all.mjs` as the sixteenth suite; all prior suites/room checks remain.
The climb browser test reads the actual keyed door state/`openness` contract, rather than treating it as a list. Offline scans of the first access export found zero physical static-body intersections along the 72.22 m climb path; both small outdoor toilet volumes still fail connected-floor planning and require further entry-clearance repair.
`climbing.mjs` approaches/returns along the clear side of the actual foremast base and Soldier Dock stair opening, rather than walking through the mast platform. `roomRoutes.mjs` computes its optional NAV_DEBUG bounds with a bounded reduction; spreading the enlarged floor-node list exceeded JavaScript's argument limit. Planning/reachability constraints remain unchanged.
`assets.test.mjs` adds retained-red/white scroll-border extent checks, actual lookout floor/ladder endpoints, dense exported static collision checks for the complete human body/hatch opening, and both outdoor toilet entry level/orientation checks. These structural assertions supplement, rather than replace, real browser access tests.
The first physical climb reached the closed hatch with no recovery, but its two-millimetre collision stop was inside the moving hatch's two-centimetre occupancy margin. `PlayerController` now sweeps climbing with the shared `CONTACT_GAP` added to body clearance, allowing the hatch to open safely overhead without changing the player's configured dimensions. `climbing.mjs` reports completed checkpoints and records current keyed hatch/climb state on failure. The two outdoor toilet doors/openings widen together to a normal 1.10 m entrance; their former frame clearance fell between the 0.20 m planner columns. Body/floor/grid constraints remain unchanged.
`docs/review-findings.md` adds the completed construction follow-up (10/14 suites; build pass), exact remaining failures, inspected construction2 images and first access-generation evidence/failed gates. These dated results describe their respective asset generations; they do not supersede later validation or constitute completion.
Further physical review: `add_fittings()` places each outdoor toilet doorway on the outer side of its under-stair shell, where the lawn approach avoids the low treads; all walls/leaf/frame metadata share that plane. The front and inner walls remain solid. `assets.test.mjs` checks this outer-facing entry station. The second climb reached/opened its hatch safely, but the fixed 700 ms test pause caught `openness=0.779` while the occupancy interlock slowed movement overhead. `climbing.mjs:toggleHatch(open)` now waits for the actual requested lid state (four-second timeout), retaining the full-open/closed assertions and real E input.

October 2 physical hatch correction: `sunny_interiors.hatch()` cuts overlapping authored walking/ceiling layers at the lid level after all preceding landing geometry exists, keeps the actual stair treads, and tags the aperture columns to exclude artificial edge guards. The four older callers in `sunny_exterior.add_fittings()` now align their lids over the upper stair flight, leaving the exit plate beside the opening rather than directly underneath it. Exact 1.50 m alignment offsets are a human-clearance reconstruction within the same reference access station, not SBS dimensions. `assets.test.mjs` requires every open sliding hatch centre to lack a static floor at its lid height. The current outer-toilet export passes 86 asset checks and all 25 room/9 stair/2 fore-approach collision routes; real controller evidence is still required. New hatch source changes await a coherent rebuild.

The first real-hole export passes 87 structural checks; floor-route checks expose the short cross-frame obstruction over descending upper treads. `add_fittings.stair_hatch()` sizes the three exterior access lids to their existing 2.10 m headroom slots, with normal 1.35/1.40 m widths and exit landings beside the openings. These 3–4 m stairwell covers are clearance reconstructions; the supplied ladder-hatch drawings do not specify these dimensions. Storage access remains a paused interior mismatch, not an exterior acceptance claim. `PlayerController` uses `climbExitHold` to require release of W/S after an automatic climb exit; a held climb key previously continued into walking at 3.4 m/s while the bounded-climb journey sampled its endpoint. The existing continuous ascent/return test verifies bounded arrival and ordinary subsequent walking.
`doors.js:createDoorSystem().nearestHatch()` measures reach to the actual oriented opening rectangle, retaining its two-metre reach/vertical band. This makes longer stairwell lids operable from the landing rather than requiring the player to occupy the lid. `doors.test.mjs` checks reachable edges and rejects edges 2.10 m away for every actual hatch, while retaining visual/collider and occupancy checks.
`roomRoutes.mjs:roomRoutes().pathBetween(a,b)` exposes a compressed connection between two supported poses using its unchanged physical-body grid checks. `tests/exteriorAccess.mjs` uses these plans for actual W/E journeys from spawn through both outdoor toilet thresholds and back, then down/return through each of the three exterior stair-hatch openings. Local `read()` reads actual controller/HUD/opening state, `toggle()` presses E only in reach and waits for real full-open/shut state, `walk()` follows the body-checked path without placement/recovery and opens only real nearby hatches, and `to()` plans from the current physical pose. It records traces, normal-eye descent/return screenshots and failure evidence. `run-all.mjs` adds this seventeenth suite while retaining every previous room/stair assertion; its header/main inventory reflects the current count.
The three exterior headroom boundaries include the retained 0.60 m floor-proxy underside, normal body height and tread/radius overlap (2.70 m envelope); the earlier 2.10 m volume cleared visible wood but still left exported ceiling collision over the descent. No collision depth/body/step tolerance is weakened. The first outdoor browser run passed both toilet thresholds and returns. `DoorSystem` exposes a read-only `near` getter for its actual system/player pose; `exteriorAccess.read()` uses it rather than importing a second dev-HMR Zustand module instance, which returned a separate empty HUD state. The resulting 3.7–5.0 m stairwell covers are functional reconstruction choices, still a reference mismatch to review against the supplied ladder-hatch drawings.
`exteriorAccess.mjs` turns the normal-height camera back toward the actual outdoor doorway before its threshold screenshot, avoiding a wall filling the evidence frame; walking/controller state is unchanged.
`docs/exterior-feature-ledger.md` retains the October 1 discrepancy baseline and adds all 42 current implementation/reference-boundary rows, current measured envelope/counts and generation-specific validation. Unverified hidden details, functional stair-cover reconstruction, paused Storage access and room-side mismatches remain explicit. Documentation only; no functions.
`exteriorAccess.mjs:flight(name,descending)` reaches the gallery/garden hatches via actual exported outside tread flights, then returns down the same flights. The previous shortest floor-grid approach chose the interior Aquarium stair beneath a deliberately closed test lid, so it could not reach the designated upper starting stance. Hatch tests now start from the exterior floor via the real aft/garden stairs, retain actual E checks and reverse the same approach path; no placement/rescue or body/tolerance changes.
`docs/to-do.md` replaces its stale first-repair handoff with the current coherent export, new physical hatch/lookout/toilet repairs, 87 structural checks, 25-room/8-of-9-flight planning result, actual outdoor walking checkpoints, last complete 10/14 suite and still-required 17-suite verification. It explicitly retains exterior-first ordering and current visible/hidden accuracy boundaries. Documentation only; no functions.
The lookout ascent now reaches the real floor at bounded speed with no recovery. `PlayerController` clears `climbExitHold` on the actual W/S keyup event, as well as deliberate placement/reset, so a quick release/repress between render frames does not remain stuck. Local `releaseClimb(e)` joins the existing cleaned-up input listeners. The full reverse descent remains to be verified; no walking tolerance changes.

Lookout return-floor contact repair: `flightCollision.js` exports its existing 2 mm `FLIGHT_CONTACT_EPSILON` without changing sweep behaviour. `climbing.js:reachedClimbPoint(position,point)` accepts that physical separation plus 0.1 mm for Float32 exported coordinates. The actual lookout floor is 0.027 mm above the metadata path; the old strict 2 mm comparison could never commit the first return step after sweeping against the floor. `PlayerController` uses this reach predicate only after the same bounded collision sweep; player dimensions, velocity, hatch solidity and journey assertions are unchanged. `climbing.test.mjs` covers the measured floor contact and rejects an unreached 2 cm proposal. The complete actual ascent/return journey must verify the repair.

Lookout hatch descent interaction: `doors.js:stepClear()` uses the swept union of endpoint bounds for horizontally translated flat hatches, retaining lateral contact clearance and rejecting actual vertical body overlap. Horizontal lid travel no longer inflates an imaginary vertical swept volume above its top. This lets a lid withdraw beneath supported feet or above a climber's head without moving through the body; closing remains blocked when the body crosses the hatch plane. All swing/vertical movement safety retains the earlier conservative sweep. The stale header describing lids as non-floor colliders is corrected to the existing actual supporting-footprint behaviour. `doors.test.mjs` retains every exported-opening assertion, tests actual lookout opening beneath feet and checks every closing frame against a standing body in the shaft, including the blocked state.
`exteriorAccess.mjs:view(target)` aims its descent/return evidence at the actual next tread/closed lid from the unchanged configured `EYE_HEIGHT_M` position. This makes the aperture/cover visible in normal-eye screenshots, with no placement or inspection-mode substitution.

Pending next export, authored while the complete current-generation suite remains stable: `sunny_accuracy.fit_scroll_paint()` now replaces the old inflated red-panel volume with a 0.10 m skin fitted to measured white-outline cross-sections and the retained source red silhouette. ### `tools/sunny_surfaces.py` — exterior surface readiness
- `floor_soffit(obj,thickness)` duplicates the actual cut floor downward, preserves every top/material/aperture, adds a cream underside and boundary walls, and tags measured visual depth. It does not lower the walking plane or change room furnishings. Current selection is exposed AftEntryDeck, Library-tier hatch floor and the two new stern access floors.
- `smooth_painted_normals(obj,crease_degrees)` groups coincident source positions, averages unique adjacent face directions only inside the world-space crease threshold, and writes custom loop normals in object space. It preserves vertices, indices, paint materials and real creases; source curves and named exterior lion/anchor/lookout/barrel/platform parts regain shading lost to face-wise paint/cuts.
- `prepare_exterior_surfaces(objects,vertex_bbox)` creates selected access undersides, runs single-owner clipping on walking tops/tagged bottoms, prepares selected nonplanar render topology, restores curved normals and writes final UVs, then returns the trimmed-face count. Material assignment happens at runtime.
`sunny_exterior.remove_exterior_overlaps()` adds stern access floor/neck, Library hatch floor and balcony access to the existing single-owner clipping order. Actual remaining wall/frame/floor faces replace overlapping bases; walking heights/holes remain. `restore_exterior()` calls surface preparation after trimming and before collision sampling/export. Fresh coherent export, broadened geometry assertions and actual images remain required; no fix is accepted from source alone.
`tests/exterior.test.mjs` broadens its existing exported area/3 mm/1 cm² coplanar check to SternAccess floors/neck, balcony floor/access and the Library-tier hatch floor; prior surface coverage and tolerances remain. `assets.test.mjs:smoothPaintSeams(name)` measures unit-normal agreement at coincident colour-boundary vertices across the actual anchor material primitives; a new assertion requires both real seams to remain smooth. Another measures all four actual 12 cm floor undersides and unchanged walking heights, supplementing the original shaft/body/room checks. These tests await coherent regeneration; they must not pass merely from metadata tags.

### `tools/sunny_uv.py` — exterior texture coordinates
- `exterior_surface(obj)` selects retained source exterior, supported fittings, exposed doors/hatches and shared outer envelopes; furnished room furniture and interior-only openings stay unselected. Existing normalized sail/emblem UVs are never replaced.
- `components(mesh)` with nested `root(i)` discovers connected leaf/branch islands in batched geometry.
- `principal_axis(points)` computes the final-metre branch/spar axis and radial basis by covariance/power iteration, independent of an object's historical origin.
- `prepare_exterior_topology(objects)` preserves source vertices/transforms/materials and replaces only selected nonplanar polygons with Blender's existing render triangles before normals/UV assignment; the source FBX remains unchanged.
- `metric_uv(obj)` writes length/circumference coordinates on poles, leaf-local normalized blade coordinates, continuous metre projections on decks/hull/source paint splits and length-oriented fitting projections. Cylinder seams unwrap per face. Exported UV/wood-family tags distinguish boards, solid grain and bark. No silhouette, position, material colour or collision dimensions change.
- `prepare_exterior_uvs(objects)` applies this only after all geometry/scale/cut operations. `sunny_surfaces.prepare_exterior_surfaces()` calls it after ownership clipping and normal repair, logs surface count, and retains the removed-face return value. Texture rendering is not wired yet; export validation is required.

### `src/systems/exteriorMaterials.js` — filtered exterior finish
- `clamp`, `fract`, `hash`, `wave` are deterministic scalar helpers for offline bitmap sampling during initial material creation.
- `sample(kind,u,v,x,y)` creates neutral albedo, metre relief and roughness samples for timber boards/solid grain/bark, painted wood, restrained metal, braided rope, lawn, individual leaf veins, fruit peel, ceramic pots, soil and canvas. Existing paint colours multiply neutral maps; there is no invented grime/weathering or new geometry.
- `texture(data,size,period,color)` configures shared 512 px RGBA textures with explicit physical repeat, trilinear mipmaps, anisotropy 8, linear normal/roughness maps and sRGB albedo.
- `exteriorTextureSet(kind)` caches generated albedo/normal/roughness maps, derives tangent normals from metre-height central differences with repeat-wrapped edges, and exposes each map's physical period. Its local `at(x,y)` reads repeat-wrapped height samples.
- `exteriorMaterial(source,{wood,kind,name})` caches physical materials by sourced colour/finish, applies board/grain/bark directions from UV tags and gives paint/metal/rope/vegetation/pots their own roughness and restrained relief. Glass stays translucent with environment reflection and no transmission render pass. No world-position grain shader is used.
`Ship()` imports this finish only for `sunny_uv_space` surfaces; existing authored room materials and legacy fallback remain. Sail graphics/deformation are unchanged; canvas detail is not wired yet. Export UV validation, visual front review and motion sampling are required before accepting this finish.

UV/material follow-up scan: `metric_uv()` skips fully clipped empty meshes and lets retained Rectangle petals/bones use their actual length direction rather than the general hull projection. `exteriorMaterial()` includes blade-normal strength in its cache key so normalized leaves cannot inherit the first metric calyx material's scale. Syntax/pattern scan precedes export acceptance; the active first UV export began before this refinement and must be checked/rebuilt if it omitted it.

First UV staging export failed on a fully clipped empty mesh before publication (`review/exterior-metric-uv-assets.log`); the published coaming generation remained intact. The explicit empty-mesh guard is in the retry `exterior-metric-uv-ready-assets.log`. `tests/assets.test.mjs` retains all 95 prior structural assertions and adds actual finite exported exterior UVs, normalized sail/room-scope isolation, one UV metre per physical deck/source-hull metre, full-height fore/main mast grain, bounded individual leaf UVs, all-map mip/anisotropy/colour-space/physical-repeat checks, unit relief normals and original-palette/nonmutated-source isolation. Local `uvWorld(i,parent)` derives actual node transforms; `deckMetres(name)` independently compares exported UV and physical triangle edges. The single runner remains 17 suites. Source syntax/scan pass; retry assets and browser finish review remain required.

UV retry coherently publishes **3,210** mapped exterior surfaces, 40.58 MB visual/21.87 MB collision. Original overlap plus exposed-coaming audits pass. The independent `deckMetres()` checker initially called typed-array `map` with Vector3 results, which coerced them to numbers; it now converts the three index IDs with `Array.from` before mapping, retaining the same physical/UV assertions. Finite UV and scope assertions already pass; complete structure and browser review remain required.

`tools/exterior_audit.mjs` now records browser console errors alongside page exceptions, so failed PBR/custom GLSL compilation cannot silently pass the matched appearance capture. Existing inventory/view/camera logic and its appearance-only evidence boundary remain unchanged. No new named functions; source syntax/scan precedes current finish capture.

First actual finish review (`review/material-base-*`) has no browser/shader errors, but UV assertions find three real defects: GLTF kept old mast UVs as TEXCOORD_0, twelve door “leaves” were misclassified as foliage by name, and six horizontal triangles within nonplanar source hull polygons inherited the wrong projection. `metric_uv()` now removes obsolete UV layers only on selected exterior surfaces and requires actual leaf/leaflight face materials for blade coordinates. New `prepare_exterior_topology(objects)` uses Blender's existing `loop_triangles` only on nonplanar polygons (>1 mm from their plane), retaining every vertex/object transform/material and the actual existing tessellation. It runs after overlap clipping and before custom normal restoration/UV assignment, so each rendered triangle receives its appropriate projection. Source FBX/normalized sail UVs/furnished room geometry stay intact. All original/new structure and UV assertions remain required; next coherent publication and motion/images must verify the fixes.
`interiorJourneys.mjs:walk()` operates a reachable closed hatch only for an actual planned transition below its lid. Ordinary level walking may cross a supported closed lid; pressing E at every nearby upper ladder landing would instead grab that ladder and interrupt the unrelated room tour. The driver retains every room/forced stair/forced ladder ascent/return and all recovery/footing/height checks, and still opens required old stair lids from below. This adapts input to the new reference topology without skipping a connection or weakening assertions.

### Front mouth construction regression — October 3, 2026

`tests/exterior.test.mjs:visit` now collects actual head/muzzle/Gaon triangles, exported material keys and hinge tags in `mouthParts`. New `mouthPose(part,amount)` transforms actual tagged lower-face triangles through the unchanged runtime jaw hinge; `frontHits(y,z,amount)` independently intersects frontal rays with actual posed triangles and sorts ownership. The new thirteenth geometry group requires H's tall mouth samples to reveal black cavity/cannon while closed painted face covers the same rays, actual opaque closed head/muzzle/jaw/chin/barrel cuts, and a deep hollow barrel/rear support rather than a flat dark disk. Normalized sample positions remain disclosed reconstruction; all twelve earlier groups/assertions/tolerances remain. Current old publication must reproduce the defect before authoring. No player/room/graphics or Environment Variables changes.

`docs/review/exterior-mouth-source-baseline.log` records the original-current-publication independent thirteenth mouth construction regression before source repair; log has no executable functions. `docs/construction-mane-review.md` preserves the preceding coherent mane generation/terminal 15/17/graphics-hold/individually inspected source and walking screenshots before the mouth batch. Markdown contains no executable functions. Source FBX and all three prior publication hashes match their recorded manifest; graphics/player/room/runtime and Environment Variables unchanged.

`tools/sunny_accuracy.py` adds `closed_face_mesh(obj,name,level,above)` to bake/weld actual source face/muzzle, bisect when requested and cap open cut edges; `face_boolean(obj,cutter,operation)` evaluates Exact source-face Boolean with retained cut material, welds/normals and refuses non-manifold output; `mouth_prism(head,bounds,centre_y,top,bottom,half_width,back)` builds H-inspired tall chamfered mouth cut using disclosed reconstructed dimensions; `gaon_barrel(front,back,centre_y,centre_z,radius,wall)` builds actual outer/inner annular barrel wall and supported black rear/retained painted rim. `lion_mechanisms` now caps original split muzzle, creates the retained thin lower face panel with black inner/cut surfaces, cuts a tall black-lined cavity while preserving source silhouette, and attaches a deep hollow barrel to the rear. All lower components/smile share a bottom-edge hinge and exported 90-degree opening angle; hidden hinge/depth/thickness remain reconstruction. All seven retained mane tags/pivots stay. No new firing mechanics, player scale, room layout or graphics/lighting/material-shader changes.

`src/components/NavigationSystem.jsx:NavigationSystem` reads each exported `sunny_jaw_angle` (old assets retain the 1.05 fallback) while applying unchanged existing J/K interpolation/control flow. `tests/exterior.test.mjs:mouthPose` reads that actual exported angle to measure runtime-equivalent posed geometry; its independent reference rays/closure/depth requirements and all original twelve groups remain. ### October 4 native aft exterior construction — integrated source batch

`tools/sunny_aft_envelope.py` restores the source-derived closed curved F2A aft envelope and supported arched sash geometry, retaining the existing cream front/door stations and real garden stair volumes. Original source is snapshotted before access cuts and transformed through the same frame; no missing cut span is interpolated. It replaces the open imported skin plus rectangular lining with a closed red-side/cream-inside shell. Functions and roles:
- `snapshot_native_house`: Capture capped uncut native polygons without modifying or exporting them.
- `frame_house_snapshot`: Apply the native house's actual layout/shift/scale, once, to its snapshot.
- `native_section_segments`: Intersect the actual source triangles with one horizontal wall section.
- `closed_native_contour`: Trace the largest fully closed source section; reject every open chain.
- `sampled_contour`: Resample along real source edges, without filling any coverage gap.
- `actual_floor_bvh`: Read actual retained room/entry/gallery floor faces, including real holes.
- `require_floor_support`: Refuse unsupported lining feet; no sampled hull/rectangle can waive this.
- `source_house_rows`: Recover every height row from complete pre-slot source, never cut skin.
- `closed_wall_mesh`: Closed source-shaped skin with fitted inner garden-stair cheeks; the original outside surface and cream/red palette remain fixed.
- `garden_stair_bounds`: Central unchanged body/headroom bounds around each actual tread.
- `garden_stair_enclosures`: Fit two inner enclosure planes and reconstructed aft blends from the actual flights.
- `stair_enclosed_inner_ring`: Fit only the stair-side inner lining and collapsed corner runs; preserve the outside source wall and every unaffected native lining vertex.
- `closed_boolean_cut`: Cut a physical closed aperture and retain its real reveal faces.
- `closed_box_cut`: Apply one exact retained door/stair body volume to the closed envelope.
- `cut_aft_stair_access`: Retain all66garden treads with finite72cm longitudinal/38cm lateral support-plus-body masks and1.95m headroom; player/stair/floor stations stay fixed.
- `build_aft_envelope`: Replace the rectangular F2 exterior with its complete native vault.
- `closed_wall_bvh`: Read both actual wall surfaces, including source curvature and openings.
- `arch_outline`: A real rectangular lower aperture with a semicircular upper head.
- `fitted_arch`: Fit each arch boundary and glazing plane to both real curved wall skins.
- `arch_prism`: Closed arched cutter/glass prism with real crown, jambs and sill.
- `closed_door_arch_cut`: Cut the two upper exterior entrances with real closed crowns inside the original depth/bottom/top limits; retain existing motion metadata.
- `curved_arch_glass`: Closed curved glazing follows the actual vault at every sash sample.
- `add_fitted_arch`: Cut a true arch and seat its curved, closed, multi-pane glazing in skin.
- `add_aft_windows`: Keep four bow windows and add reviewed side/rear curved-skin stations.
- `paint_lower_aquarium_tier`: Partition/reassign actual source timber faces within lower Aquarium bounds to cream; preserve timber below and other paint without overlays.
- `fitted_arch_pair`: Fit each lower sash boundary to both actual outer hull and inner lining snapshots; require supported depth.
- `closed_arch_reveal`: Build a closed annular tunnel physically joining both retained skins around each true arch.
- `add_lower_aquarium_arch`: Cut both opaque skins and fit closed glazing, outer frame and sash bars against supplied uncut support snapshots.
- `add_lower_aquarium_windows`: Snapshot four supports before paint/cuts; author four side/two rear sashes and disclose reconstructed stations, retaining existing front sashes/rooms/access.
